### [135] [AKSZ Descent on Manifolds with Ordinary Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02928)
*Cristian Anghel*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文在显式映射空间假设下，将经典AKSZ构造推广到带普通角的紧致定向流形上，建立了面向整个面偏序集的"面式"（facewise）构造，其中哈密顿缺陷被分解为各余一维面的原像之和。通过面关联复形组织这些缺陷，得到全复形定理：因子归一化的面式转置是一个上链映射，且二次迭代缺陷因带符号的面微分平方为零而消失。作者在四维BF理论（M=Γ×[0,1]²）上显式验证了所有四个余二维相消。此外还建立了奇异角数据的约化判据，当原始余二维后裔是预辛的且其约化Dirac结构是某个Poisson双向量场的图时，平移余切构造给出严格的二阶角理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典AKSZ构造已在带角流形上得到了BV-BFV框架下的逐层描述，但缺乏将所有层（face）统一组织起来的整体结构。本文的动机是将CMR（Cattaneo–Mnev–Reshetikhin）的极大扩展BV–BFV理论中逐层的哈密顿缺陷编码到整个面偏序集上，从而给出一个统一的、顾及符号和定向的全复形描述，并为推广到Joyce广义角提供严格的普通角基准。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 在显式映射空间假设下，对带普通角的流形给出AKSZ构造的面式（facewise）表述，每个余维度r的面携带闭二阶形式（度r-1）、度r作用和上同调向量场；(2) 将每个面（face）上的哈密顿缺陷表示为其余一维面上原像按定向关联数加权的拉回之和，从而将单层恒等式的边界项分解为带符号的连通片；(3) 引入面关联复形（face incidence complex），证明因子归一化的面式转置构成上链映射，且带符号面微分平方为零导致二次迭代缺陷消失；(4) 在M=Γ×[0,1]²上的四维BF理论中显式验证全部四个余二维相消；(5) 建立约化判据：若原始余二维后裔为预辛的且约化Dirac结构是Poisson双向量场的图，则平移余切构造给出严格的二阶角理论。

Result: 主要结果包括：面式公式将哈密顿缺陷分解为带符号的连通片之和；全复形定理表明因子归一化的面式转置是上链映射（闭目标形式转置为上循环）；带符号面微分平方为零保证二次迭代缺陷消失；在四维BF理论中显式验证了所有四个余二维相消关系；建立了约化判据，保证在预辛条件及Poisson双向量场图结构下可构造严格的二阶角理论。

Conclusion: 本文提供了带普通角流形上AKSZ构造的统一面式表述，通过面关联复形将逐层的BV-BFV结构组织为全复形定理，并验证了低维示例。该框架为未来推广到Joyce广义角（非普通角）理论提供了严格的基准和判据。

Abstract: Under an explicit formal mapping-space hypothesis, we develop a facewise formulation of the classical AKSZ construction on compact oriented manifolds with ordinary corners. The codimension-$r$ data---a mapping space carrying a closed two-form of degree $r-1$, an action of degree $r$, and a cohomological vector field---and the modified Batalin--Vilkovisky/Batalin--Fradkin--Vilkovisky Hamiltonian identity relating consecutive strata are those of the maximally extended BV--BFV theory of Cattaneo--Mnev--Reshetikhin. What is added here is the organization over the entire face poset: the Hamiltonian defect on a face is the sum of the pullbacks of the primitives on its codimension-one faces, weighted by the orientation incidence numbers, so that the boundary term of the single-stratum identity is resolved into its connected pieces with signs.
  Organizing these defects by the face incidence complex yields a total-complex theorem: factorially normalized facewise transgression is a cochain map, so closed target forms transgress to cocycles, and the twice-iterated defect vanishes because the signed face differential squares to zero. We verify all four codimension-two cancellations explicitly for four-dimensional BF theory on $M=Γ\times[0,1]^2$.
  We also establish a reduction criterion for singular corner data. If a raw codimension-two descendant is presymplectic and its reduced Dirac structure is the graph of a Poisson bivector, the shifted cotangent construction gives a canonical strict degree-two corner theory. The passage from a reduced Poisson bivector to a strict corner theory is already recorded in \cite{CFT2026}; what is isolated here is the hypothesis under which it applies, and its relation to the face-incidence structure. The construction provides a rigorous ordinary-corner benchmark for extensions of AKSZ descent to Joyce generalized corners.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Homogeneous spaces with two equivalent isotropy summands develop positive Ricci curvature under Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02840)
*Eric Cochran,Arseny Mingajev,Lawrence Mouillé,Nazia Valiyakath*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通齐性空间 G/H 上的规范化 Ricci 流，其中迷向表示恰好分解为两个等价的不可约子表示。证明了每个 G-不变度量都会演化到具有正 Ricci 曲率的度量，且正 Ricci 曲率的 G-不变度量族在流下是前向不变的。证明依赖于固定体积 G-不变度量族的相图可被显式可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究规范化 Ricci 流在特定齐性空间上的长期行为，特别是关于 Ricci 曲率符号的演化，这类问题在 Ricci 流的几何分析中具有重要理论意义。通过对迷向表示结构较为简单的空间（恰好两个等价不可约子表示）进行研究，旨在获得可显式刻画的动力学结果。

Method: 利用齐性空间 G/H 的迷向表示恰好分裂为两个等价不可约子表示这一特殊结构，将 G-不变度量族的相空间简化为可显式可视化的常微分方程系统。通过分析固定体积度量族的相图（phase portrait），证明所有轨线最终进入正 Ricci 曲率区域，并证明该区域在规范化的 Ricci 流下是前向不变的。

Result: 证明了在单连通齐性空间 G/H（其中迷向表示分裂为两个等价不可约子表示）上，任意 G-不变度量在规范化 Ricci 流下都会演化到具有正 Ricci 曲率的度量；同时证明了正 Ricci 曲率的 G-不变度量集合在流下是前向不变的。

Conclusion: 这类齐性空间上的规范化 Ricci 流具有"正 Ricci 曲率吸引"的动力学行为：所有 G-不变度量最终进入且无法逃离正 Ricci 曲率区域。这为更一般齐性空间上 Ricci 流的曲率演化和长期收敛性提供了新的洞见。

Abstract: We study normalized Ricci flow on simply connected homogeneous spaces G/H for which the isotropy representation splits into exactly two equivalent irreducible subrepresentations. We prove that every G-invariant metric evolves to one with positive Ricci curvature, and that the family of G-invariant metrics with positive Ricci curvature is forward-invariant under the flow. The proof relies on the fact that the phase portrait of the family of fixed-volume G-invariant metrics can be explicitly visualized.

</details>


### [137] [Geodesic string counting invariants and arithmetic of multiplicities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02857)
*Yasha Savelyev*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于测地流的Fuller指标研究完备Riemann-Finsler流形上测地弦（封闭测地线的重参数化等价类）的有理数值计数，主要概念性结果是一个乘积型公式。结合KAM理论，该公式可导出：在足够C^∞接近平坦度量的环面T²上，对一般Finsler度量及素数p，若某自由同伦类存在多重性能被p整除的测地弦，则必存在另一个。此外还获得映射环和平坦丛中闭测地线计数的算术约束，以及负截面曲率度量存在性的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统闭测地线的计数通常作为整数计数，但几何测地线（即重参数化等类）的计数需要更精细的工具。作者希望通过Fuller指标建立一种有理数计数的体系，从而揭示测地弦计数与拓扑、动力学（KAM理论）以及算术结构之间的深层联系，并为猜想中的无穷维商栈[LX/S^1]上的orbifold Morse同调提供"影子"（shadow）。

Method: 作者引入基于测地流Fuller指标的测地弦有理计数方法，建立乘积型公式作为核心工具。随后将Fuller指标计数与KAM理论相结合，应用于环面T²上的Finsler度量（足够接近平坦度量），通过同伦类测地弦的多重性（multiplicity）与素数p之间的整除性关系导出存在性结论。同时利用该框架分析映射环和平坦丛结构的算术约束。

Result: 得到三个主要结果：（1）乘积公式与样本现象：T²上一般Finsler度量接近平坦时，若某自由同伦类β存在多重性被p整除的测地弦，则必然存在另一个（不能唯一）；（2）映射环和平坦丛中闭测地线计数满足算术约束；（3）对负截面曲率度量的存在性给出约束。这些计数被视为无穷维商栈[LX/S^1]上猜想性orbifold Morse同调的"影子"。

Conclusion: 测地弦的有理计数提供了比经典整数计数更丰富的几何信息，其乘积型公式与KAM理论结合揭示了Finsler度量下测地弦多重性的内在规律。该框架不仅适用于具体流形（如T²），还赋予闭测地线计数算术结构，并指向一个更宏大的orbifold Morse同调理论作为深层解释。

Abstract: We study rational valued counts of geodesic strings (reparametrization equivalence classes of closed geodesics) for complete Riemann-Finsler manifolds, based on the Fuller index of the geodesic flow. The main conceptual result is a product type formula for these counts. Combined with aspects of KAM theory, it yields the following sample phenomenon. Let $g$ be a generic Finsler metric on $T ^{2}$, sufficiently $C ^{\infty }$-close to a flat metric, and fix a prime $p$ and a nontrivial free homotopy class $β$. If there is a class $β$ $g$-geodesic string with multiplicity divisible by $p$, then there is another one. We also obtain arithmetic constraints on counts of closed geodesics in mapping tori and flat bundles, and constraints on the existence of negative sectional curvature metrics. These counts can be understood as a shadow of a conjectural orbifold Morse homology of the infinite-dimensional quotient stack $[LX/S^1]$.

</details>


### [138] [Joint spectrum of the sub-Laplacian and the Reeb vector field on three-dimensional Heisenberg Bieberbach manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03265)
*Yoshiaki Suzuki,Yuya Takeuchi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维Heisenberg Bieberbach流形上的子Laplacian与Reeb向量场的联合谱，并利用theta函数和有限群特征论显式计算了联合谱的重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heisenberg Bieberbach流形是Heisenberg群的紧商空间，其上的谱分析是亚黎曼几何和调和分析的重要课题。理解子Laplacian与Reeb向量场的联合谱及其重数，有助于深入刻画这类流形的几何与分析性质。

Method: 利用theta函数工具和有限群的特征理论，对三维Heisenberg Bieberbach流形上子Laplacian与Reeb向量场的联合谱进行显式计算，特别是精确推导各谱点的重数。

Result: 给出了三维Heisenberg Bieberbach流形上子Laplacian与Reeb向量场联合谱的完整描述，并显式计算了谱的重数。

Conclusion: 通过theta函数与有限群特征论的结合，成功实现了对Heisenberg Bieberbach流形联合谱重数的精确计算，为该类流形上的谱理论研究提供了系统方法。

Abstract: A Heisenberg Bieberbach manifold is a compact quotient of the Heisenberg group by a discrete torsion-free subgroup of its pseudo-Hermitian automorphism group. In this paper, we study the joint spectrum of the sub-Laplacian and the Reeb vector field on three-dimensional Heisenberg Bieberbach manifolds. In particular, we explicitly compute the multiplicities of the joint spectrum using theta functions and the character theory of finite groups.

</details>


### [139] [Complete Minimal Submanifolds of the Non-Compact Duals to the Classical Compact Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03354)
*Thomas Jack Munn*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在非紧致对偶空间（经典紧致李群的对称空间对偶）中构造了新的余维数为二的完备极小子流形族，具体涵盖SO(n)\SO(n)、SL(n,C)/SU(n)和Sp(n,C)/Sp(n)三类对称空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非紧致对称空间中构造新的完备极小子流形是微分几何的核心问题之一，此前在该类空间中已知的完备极小子流形族较为有限，作者旨在通过新的构造方法扩展这一领域。

Method: 作者通过在经典紧致李群的非紧致对偶空间（即对称空间SO(n)\SO(n)、SL(n,C)/SU(n)和Sp(n,C)/Sp(n)）中构造新的族来获得余维数为二的完备极小子流形。

Result: 成功构造了三类非紧致对偶空间中余维数为二的新的完备极小子流形族。

Conclusion: 该工作丰富了非紧致对称空间中完备极小子流形的分类与构造理论，为后续研究提供了新的构造方法和实例。

Abstract: We construct new families of complete minimal submanifolds of codimension two in the non-compact duals to the classical compact Lie groups $\SOC n/\SO n$, $\SLC n/\SU n$ and $\SpC n/\Sp n$.

</details>


### [140] [Lower Bound for Weighted Intermediate Ricci Curvature and Tensorial Entropy Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03405)
*Chao-Ming Lin,Kai-Hsiang Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入了加权形式的中间Ricci曲率，建立了其下界的若干等价刻画，并以此为工具推广了Aishwarya-Rotem-Shenfeld的结果，推导出热流的固有维度演化变分不等式及相应的Wasserstein收缩估计，同时与Ketterer-Mondino通过低维最优输运给出的刻画进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间Ricci曲率（intermediate Ricci curvature）在几何与度量测度空间中扮演重要角色，但其加权形式及其下界的刻画尚不完善。本文旨在建立加权中间Ricci曲率下界的等价刻画，并利用这些刻画推广已有的热流收缩性结果，从而深化对曲率约束下热流行为及最优输运结构的理解。

Method: 引入加权中间Ricci曲率的定义，通过建立其下界的多种等价刻画（可能涉及曲率张量、Ricci部分、切空间方向等不同视角），将曲率条件转化为积分/变分不等式形式，进而应用于热流（heat flow）的分析，推导固有维度的演化变分不等式与Wasserstein收缩估计。

Result: 成功建立了加权中间Ricci曲率下界的若干等价刻画，并将其用于推广Aishwarya-Rotem-Shenfeld的工作，得到了热流在固有维度意义下的演化变分不等式和Wasserstein收缩估计；同时与Ketterer-Mondino基于低维最优输运的刻画进行了比较，阐明了两种方法之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本文为加权中间Ricci曲率提供了系统性刻画，拓展了其应用范围，特别是给出了热流的Wasserstein收缩与演化变分不等式的推广形式，并厘清了与已有最优输运方法的联系，为后续研究曲率维数条件与输运几何的交叉问题奠定了新基础。

Abstract: We introduce a weighted version of intermediate Ricci curvature and establish several equivalent characterizations of its lower bound. As an application, we generalize the results of Aishwarya--Rotem--Shenfeld [arXiv:2509.23399v1] by deriving intrinsic-dimensional evolution variational inequalities and the corresponding Wasserstein contraction estimates for the heat flow. We also compare our characterization with that of Ketterer--Mondino [arXiv:1610.03339v3] via lower-dimensional optimal transport.

</details>


### [141] [Variation formulas for mean curvature functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03431)
*Simone Verzellesi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在任意黎曼流形中建立了一般平均曲率泛函在任意变分下的一阶和二阶变分公式，并通过黎曼逼近方案在亚黎曼海森堡群中获得了相应的公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一般平均曲率泛函提供统一的变分理论框架，并从黎曼环境推广到亚黎曼海森堡群这一重要几何设定中。

Method: 在任意黎曼流形中对一般平均曲率泛函的任意变分推导一阶、二阶变分公式；随后利用黎曼逼近（Riemannian approximation）技术，将结果推广到亚黎曼海森堡群。

Result: 获得了任意黎曼流形上一般平均曲率泛函在任意变分下的一阶与二阶变分公式，并在海森堡群的亚黎曼框架下给出了对应公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般平均曲率流的变分分析提供了统一且普适的理论基础，并成功将关键结论延伸至亚黎曼几何领域。

Abstract: We establish first and second variation formulas for general mean curvature functionals under arbitrary variations in arbitrary Riemannian manifolds. We obtain corresponding formulas in the sub-Riemannian Heisenberg group through a Riemannian approximation scheme.

</details>


### [142] [Examples of Curvature Inhomogeneous Submanifolds with Constant Ricci Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03519)
*Jianquan Ge,Yuyang Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了两类具有恒定Ricci特征值的曲率非齐次黎曼流形：一类源自Einstein扭曲积，具有两个不同的Ricci特征值；另一类源自Riemannian Schwarzschild–Tangherlini流形，具有k+1个不同Ricci特征值，且分别达到最小浸入/嵌入余维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有恒定Ricci特征值但曲率非齐次的黎曼流形，并研究其最小等距浸入/嵌入的余维数问题，是黎曼几何中关于流形局部嵌入与特征值结构之间关系的重要探索。

Method: 第一类通过Einstein扭曲积构造，得到具有两个不同Ricci特征值的流形，并证明其存在最小余维数为2的局部等距浸入。第二类基于Riemannian Schwarzschild–Tangherlini流形构造，得到具有k+1个不同Ricci特征值的流形，并证明其在适配乘积类内存在最小余维数为k+2的等距嵌入。

Result: 构造出两类曲率非齐次但Ricci特征值恒定的黎曼流形，分别具有2个和k+1个不同Ricci特征值，并给出相应最小余维数的等距浸入/嵌入结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使Ricci特征值保持恒定，流形的曲率仍可非齐次，且不同构造来源的流形具有不同的最小嵌入余维数，体现了Ricci特征值结构与流形几何嵌入性质之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We construct two families of curvature inhomogeneous Riemannian manifolds with constant Ricci eigenvalues. The first, derived from the Einstein warped products, has two distinct Ricci eigenvalues and admits a local isometric immersion of minimum codimension two. The second, arising from the Riemannian Schwarzschild--Tangherlini manifold, has $k+1$ distinct Ricci eigenvalues and admits an isometric embedding of codimension $k+2$, which is the smallest within the adapted product class.

</details>


### [143] [CscK metrics on birational models of projective varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03572)
*Zakarias Sjöström Dyrefelt*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明每个复射影簇都双有理于一个光滑射影流形，该流形承载常标量曲率Kähler（cscK）度量。通过对一般Lefschetz铅笔在充分正线性系统中的余维二基点进行解消，可构造出任意光滑射影簇的双有理cscK模型，其极化显式给出，即便初始簇不稳定也适用。在二维情形，这证明了众所周知的猜想：任何复射影曲面在足够多点处爆破后均承认cscK度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cscK度量是Kähler几何中重要的规范度量，其存在性与射影簇的代数几何稳定性（如K-稳定性）密切相关。然而，并非所有射影簇都承载cscK度量，此前缺乏通用的构造方法。本文旨在证明所有复射影簇都能通过双有理变换获得承载cscK度量的光滑模型，从而在双有理几何与微分几何之间建立更深的联系。

Method: 选取充分正的线性系统中一般Lefschetz铅笔，其基点集合余维为二。通过解消该基点，得到射影簇的光滑双有理模型。剩余奇异性可以通过这样的手段被处理，从而构造一个显式的cscK极化，并证明该极化下退化稳定性条件成立，进而应用cscK度量的存在性定理。核心在于显式给出cscK极化并验证对应的K-稳定性条件。

Result: 证明了每个复射影簇双有理等价于承载cscK度量的光滑射影流形。特别地，在二维情形证明了对任意复射影曲面在充分多点处爆破后均承认cscK度量（即爆破曲面上的cscK猜想成立）。构造的cscK极化是显式的，即使初始射影簇在K-稳定性意义下不稳定，构造仍然有效，产生一个"cscK模型"。

Conclusion: 本文给出了cscK度量的全新且普遍的构造方法，证明cscK度量在双有理等价类中广泛存在。这既深化了对cscK度量存在性的理解，也建立了代数几何（双有理几何）与微分几何（Kähler度量）之间的新联系，并解决了二维情形下的长期猜想。

Abstract: We prove that every complex projective variety is birational to a smooth projective manifold admitting a constant scalar curvature Kähler (cscK) metric. For any smooth projective variety, a birational cscK model is obtained by resolving the codimension two base locus of a general Lefschetz pencil in a sufficiently positive linear system. The cscK polarization is given explicitly, producing a cscK model also when the initial variety is unstable. In dimension two this proves the folklore conjecture that the blowup of any complex projective surface in enough points admits cscK metrics.

</details>


### [144] [On Homogeneous Kähler Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03785)
*Antonio De Nicola,Fabrizio Pugliese,Luca Vitagliano*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了齐次Kähler结构相比Sasakian结构的推广程度，通过线丛分析框定了这一推广的边界，并展示了修改齐次性条件还可涵盖co-Kähler结构及其自然推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grabowski等人提出的齐次Kähler结构框架将Sasakian几何从余定向切触流形推广到非必然余定向的切触结构，但这一推广的精确范围尚不明确，需要系统地刻画其边界。

Method: 通过对主\mathbb{R}^\times-丛P上Kähler结构与切触几何之间所有相容性条件进行详细分析，并利用与P自然关联的线丛来系统刻画齐次Kähler结构的推广程度；同时通过修改Kähler结构上的齐次性条件来纳入co-Kähler结构及其推广。

Result: 精确刻画了齐次Kähler结构推广Sasakian结构的程度边界，并通过调整齐次性条件使该框架能够统一涵盖co-Kähler结构及其自然推广。

Conclusion: 齐次Kähler结构确实比Sasakian结构更为一般，但其推广范围可通过线丛框架精确定界；该框架经适当修改后还能自然包含co-Kähler结构，显示了更强的统一性。

Abstract: The cone $M \times \mathbb{R}_+$ over a Sasakian manifold $M$ is equipped with a canonical Kähler structure with specific homogeneity properties with respect to the $\mathbb{R}_+$ coordinate. This Kähler structure completely encodes the underlying Sasakian structure. Recently, Grabowski, Grabowska and Mohseni provided a broader conceptual framework for this phenomenon via homogeneous Kähler structures, i.e. Kähler structures on a principal $\mathbb{R}^\times$-bundle $P$ satisfying similar homogeneity properties. This approach successfully extends Sasakian geometry from cooriented contact manifolds (where $P$ is a trivial principal bundle) to non-necessarily coorientable contact structures (where $P$ is non-necessarily trivial). Homogeneous Kähler structures are genuinely more general than Sasakian structures and this note precisely characterizes the extent of this generalization. This is achieved through a detailed analysis of all the involved compatibilities in terms of the line bundle tautologically associated to $P$. We also show that modifying the homogeneity condition on the Kähler structure allows this framework to encompass co-Kähler structures and a natural generalization of those as well.

</details>


### [145] [A numerical criterion for complex Hessian type equations on projective manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03815)
*Gao Chen,Sijie Nie,Yulun Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个Nakai-Moishezon型判别准则，适用于射影流形上由强严格右Noetherian多项式（次数为n）所关联的复Hessian型方程，并对任意次数的严格右Noetherian多项式证明了统一的Nakai-Moishezon型判别准则。该结果涵盖了复Hessian方程和Hessian商方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复Hessian型方程在几何分析中具有重要地位，其可解性判别准则（特别是Nakai-Moishezon型条件）是核心问题。已有的结果可能仅适用于特定类型的方程，本文旨在推广这一判别准则到更广泛的方程类别，统一处理复Hessian方程和Hessian商方程。

Method: 作者引入了"强严格右Noetherian"多项式的概念，通过代数性质对度数为n的相关多项式建立了Nakai-Moishezon型判别准则；随后对任意次数的严格右Noetherian多项式建立了统一的判别准则，采用了统一的证明框架。

Result: 对次数为n的强严格右Noetherian多项式，给出了射影流形上对应复Hessian型方程解的Nakai-Moishezon型判别准则；对任意次数的严格右Noetherian多项式，给出了统一的判别准则，并证明了该框架涵盖了复Hessian方程和Hessian商方程。

Conclusion: 该结果将经典的Nakai-Moishezon型判别准则推广到了一个包含复Hessian方程和Hessian商方程的更大方程类别，为这类方程的可解性提供了统一的判别条件。

Abstract: We prove a Nakai-Moishezon-type criterion for complex Hessian-type equations on projective manifolds whose associated degree-$n$ polynomials are strongly strictly right-Noetherian. For strictly right-Noetherian polynomials of arbitrary degree, we prove a uniform Nakai-Moishezon-type criterion. This class includes the complex Hessian and Hessian quotient equations.

</details>


### [146] [New Complex-Valued Hamonic Morphisms via Rational Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03905)
*Sigmundur Gudmundsson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文推广了复值本征族构造方法，将其从多项式解推广到有理指数解，从而获得更多非线性微分几何问题的新解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复值本征族方法已被用于求解微分几何中的困难非线性问题，但现有构造依赖多项式解，限制了解空间的丰富性。本文旨在通过引入有理指数解来推广该构造方法，以获得更多新解。

Method: 将原有的复值本征族构造方法从多项式解推广至具有有理指数的解形式，利用有理指数扩展解的搜索空间，从而构造出更广泛的解族。

Result: 通过推广至有理指数解，获得了所研究非线性问题的大量新解。

Conclusion: 有理指数推广为复值本征族方法提供了更广泛的解构造途径，显著丰富了非线性微分几何问题的解空间。

Abstract: The method of complex-valued eigenfamilies has been used to solve difficult non-linear problems in differential geometry. The purpose of this note is to generalise the construction method from the use of polynomial solutions to those obtained by rational exponents. This gives many new solutions to the problem under investigation.

</details>


### [147] [On the Brunn-Minkowski inequality for $q$-th dual quermassintegrals with $q>n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03949)
*Haizhong Li,Yao Wan,Yijia Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了q>n时第q阶对偶拟质量积分（dual quermassintegrals）的Brunn-Minkowski不等式。结果表明该不等式对任意凸体在q>n时失效，对中心对称凸体在q>n+2时失效；但在q=n+2端点情形对中心对称凸体成立（利用Hadwiger关于极转动惯量的不等式）；对无条件凸体在0<q≤n+1全范围内成立（通过奇异加权Reilly公式和坐标切片Hardy不等式）。作为应用，推导了对偶曲率测量的若干唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brunn-Minkowski不等式是凸几何中的核心不等式之一，Sadovsky和Zhang近期提出了关于q阶对偶拟质量积分（q>n）的Brunn-Minkowski不等式问题。该问题涉及对偶混合体积理论中高维参数情形的刻画，其解决对于理解对偶曲率测量与凸体的唯一性问题具有重要意义。

Method: 本文采用多种方法：(1) 二阶变分论证与降维构造，用于证明不等式失效的反例；(2) Hadwiger关于极转动惯量的不等式，用于证明端点情形q=n+2对中心对称凸体成立；(3) 奇异加权Reilly公式与坐标切片Hardy不等式，用于处理无条件凸体在全范围0<q≤n+1的情形。

Result: (1) 当q>n时，不等式对任意凸体失效；当q>n+2时，对中心对称凸体也失效。(2) 端点情形q=n+2对中心对称凸体成立。(3) 对无条件凸体，不等式在0<q≤n+1全范围内成立。(4) 作为应用，得到对偶曲率测量的唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了q阶对偶拟质量积分Brunn-Minkowski不等式的成立与失效范围，区分了不同凸体类别（任意凸体、中心对称凸体、无条件凸体）下的临界指标，并通过对偶曲率测量的唯一性结果展示了其应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Brunn-Minkowski inequality for $q$-th dual quermassintegrals with $q>n$. This problem was recently posed by Sadovsky and Zhang. First, by a second variation argument and a dimension reduction construction, we show that the inequality fails for arbitrary convex bodies when $q>n$, and fails even in the origin-symmetric class when $q>n+2$. Secondly, we prove the endpoint case $q=n+2$ for origin-symmetric convex bodies via Hadwiger's inequality for the polar moment of inertia. Finally, for unconditional convex bodies, we establish the inequality in the full range $0<q\le n+1$ by using a singular weighted Reilly formula and a coordinate-slice Hardy inequality. As applications, we derive several uniqueness results for the corresponding dual curvature measures.

</details>


### [148] [Analysis on surfaces with locally bounded integral curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03982)
*Sebastian Boldt,Batu Güneysu,Maxime Marot*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有局部有界积分曲率（BIC）的完备奇异曲面（可能非紧致）作为带二维Hausdorff测度的度量测度空间，具有无穷小Hilbert性、局部加倍性和局部Poincaré不等式，从而获得Cheeger Laplacian的联合Hölder连续热核；在曲率测度的负部满足Dynkin型条件时，曲面与下界曲率测度的BIC曲面双Lipschitz等价，得到全局版本的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异曲面的几何与分析性质是几何分析中的基础问题。BIC曲面作为一类广义曲率曲面，其分析性质（如热核存在性、Hilbert性等）尚未被系统建立。本文旨在填补这一空白，将度量测度空间理论中的分析工具应用于BIC曲面。

Method: 将BIC曲面视为带有二维Hausdorff测度的度量测度空间，利用度量测度空间理论中的分析框架（Cheeger能量、加倍测度、Poincaré不等式等）进行验证；并通过Dynkin型条件（关于曲率测度负部的条件）建立与下界曲率BIC曲面之间的双Lipschitz等价，以此传递全局性质。

Result: 证明了BIC曲面是无穷小Hilbert的、局部加倍且满足局部Poincaré不等式，进而推出Cheeger Laplacian存在联合Hölder连续的热核。在Dynkin型条件下，得到全局版本的上述结果（通过双Lipschitz等价降低到曲率有下界的情形）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了BIC曲面作为度量测度空间的良好分析结构，并给出了热核的Hölder正则性以及全局化条件，为奇异曲面上的偏微分方程与几何分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove several analytic results on (possibly noncompact) complete singular surfaces having locally bounded integral curvature (in short: BIC surfaces). Regarding these as metric measure spaces with the 2-dimensional Hausdorff measure, we show that these are infinitesimally Hilbertian, locally doubling and satisfy a local Poincaré inequality. In particular, this entails the existence of a jointly Hölder continuous heat kernel for the Cheeger Laplacian. Assuming that the negative part of the curvature measure of a BIC surface satisfies a Dynkin-type condition, we show that the surface is bi-Lipschitz equivalent to a BIC surface with a lower bounded curvature measure, entailing global variants of the aforementioned results.

</details>

### [149] [Overlapping resonance branches of a gauge-invariant Lorentz-violating massive vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02655)
*Zurab Kepuladze*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在单个优选四矢构成的规范不变、CPT偶数、维数为四的洛伦兹破坏能动量算子下，阿贝尔有质量矢量场的传播和共振行为。无质量理论中传播子含有一个额外的代数极点，但与守恒流解耦，保留两个物理自由度；自发破缺U(1)对称性后，三个矢量极化全部变为物理态，但分成两个正交色散分支，分别携带两个和一个物理极化态。这种分裂在Dyson重求和下保持不变，其分裂极结构和双共振行为无法通过有限阶洛伦兹破坏微扰插入理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解洛伦兹破坏背景下有质量矢量场的物理自由度重新分配和共振现象，特别是分裂极点结构如何影响标准模型过程的可观测量，以及这种非微扰效应在不同背景几何下的表现差异。

Method: 论文采用场论分析方法：分析含洛伦兹破坏动能项的传播子结构，研究其代数极点；通过自发对称破缺机制引入质量项；进行Dyson重求和验证传播子分裂结构在辐射修正下保持稳定；计算衰变率和费米子湮灭振幅；分别考察timelike、spacelike和lightlike背景矢量下的物理效应。

Result: 结果表明：无质量理论中额外极点对守恒流解耦，只有两个物理自由度；质量理论中三个极化分裂为两个正交色散分支（2+1物理极化态分配），该结构在Dyson重求和下保持；第二分支对物理过程的贡献依赖初始动量几何——无质量对头碰撞费米子在timelike背景中贡献消失，而增强的近平行构型可产生能量增强修正；spacelike和lightlike背景导致方向性和恒星时（sidered）依赖性变化。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破缺矢量场存在非微扰的分裂极点结构，产生双共振行为，该行为无法通过有限阶微扰洛伦兹破坏修正来描述；其对物理过程的观测效应与动量几何和背景矢量类型强相关，为洛伦兹对称性实验检验提供了新的可区分信号特征。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation and resonance behavior of an Abelian massive vector field in the presence of a gauge-invariant, CPT-even, dimension-four Lorentz-violating kinetic operator constructed from a single preferred four-vector. In the massless theory, the propagator contains an additional algebraic pole that decouples from conserved currents, leaving two physical degrees of freedom. After spontaneous breaking of the internal U(1) symmetry, all three vector polarizations become physical but separate into two orthogonal dispersion branches carrying two and one physical polarization states, respectively. We show that this decomposition is preserved under Dyson resummation for a transverse matter vacuum polarization and calculate the corresponding decay rates and fermion-annihilation amplitude. The split-pole structure and associated double resonance behavior cannot be understood through a finite number of perturbative Lorentz-violating insertions. The contribution of the second branch in physical processes is strongly dependent on the initial momentum geometry: it vanishes for massless head-on fermions in a timelike background, whereas boosted nearly parallel configurations can generate an energy-enhanced correction. Spacelike and lightlike backgrounds additionally lead to directional and potentially sidereal variations of the resonance signal.

</details>


### [150] [Rank and Scale Tests of Shell-Motivated Fermion-Mass Ansatze with a Constrained Neutrino Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02701)
*Yaroslav D. Krivenko-Emetov,Oleksii M. Lytvynenko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文检验了两个八参数对数型ansatz（M1和M2）对九个带电费米子Yukawa本征值在M_Z尺度上的拟合，并扩展出中微子扇区模型M3。M1含一个秩约束R1=1（实测6.662），M2通过后选择赋予各带电扇区独立离散曲率，其关系R2=1实测0.4019。在25%模型偏差下，M1和M2的加权卡方最小值分别为6.003和1.389。M3结合中微子扇区得到质量关系，并预测正常/反转质量顺序下最轻中微子质量与总质量。作者明确承认M2和M3属于探索性工作，因后选择与缺乏微观推导而未被充分验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图通过对数型参数化的质量公式来揭示带电费米子Yukawa耦合和质量谱背后的潜在规律，并借助重整化群下的维度嬗变机制为对数线性趋势提供动机。其目的是在不需要微观理论推导的情况下，探索费米子质量层级结构的唯象学描述，并进一步将模型推广到中微子扇区。

Method: 采用两个八参数对数型ansatz（M1和M2）拟合九个带电费米子Yukawa本征值（在M_Z尺度、统一重整化方案下）。M1含一个与参数无关的秩约束R1=1；M2在M1残差基础上显式后选择，为带电扇区分配独立离散曲率而不增加参数数目。M3扩展至中性扇区，共享M2的带电轻子曲率，形成12×10设计矩阵并导出第二左零关系。通过精确加权最小化计算卡方，并以NuFIT 6.0中心分裂值为输入预测中微子质量。

Result: M1给出R1(M_Z)=6.662（理论上应为1），在25%模型偏差下chi^2_min=6.003；M2给出R2(M_Z)=0.4019（理论上应为1），chi^2_min=1.389，均有一个名义自由度。M3预测正常顺序下m_lightest=1.04×10^-4 eV、sum m_nu=0.05889 eV；反转顺序下m_lightest=3.51×10^-3 eV、sum m_nu=0.10269 eV。M2和M3因后选择性质与未微观推导而仅具探索性。

Conclusion: 对数型ansatz在描述费米子质量谱方面展现了潜力，M2通过后选择显著改善了拟合优度，而M3为预测中微子绝对质量提供了具体数值。然而，作者的结论保持审慎：M2和M3属于探索性工作，由于后选择偏差和缺乏微观理论推导，其有效性尚需进一步验证，且"维度嬗变"的论证仅为动机性而非推导性依据。

Abstract: We test two eight-parameter logarithmic ansatze against the nine charged-fermion Yukawa eigenvalues evaluated in one renormalization scheme at the common scale mu = M_Z. A smooth log-linear trend is given a conditional renormalization-group motivation through dimensional transmutation in a hypothetical asymptotically free confining sector; this motivates, but does not microscopically derive, the mass formula.
  Model M1 has one parameter-independent rank constraint, R_1 = y_u y_mu^2 y_t/(y_e y_c^2 y_tau) = 1, whereas the inputs give R_1(M_Z) = 6.662. Its alternating residuals motivate the explicitly post-selected model M2, which assigns independent discrete curvatures to the charged sectors without increasing the parameter count. Its sole relation is R_2 = y_e y_mu^2 y_tau/(y_d y_s^2 y_b) = 1, while R_2(M_Z) = 0.4019. For an illustrative uncalibrated 25 percent relative model discrepancy per state, the exact weighted minima are chi^2_min = 6.003 for M1 and 1.389 for M2, with one nominal degree of freedom.
  A constrained neutral-sector extension M3 gives the neutrino mass-eigenstate triplet its own offset and slope but shares the M2 charged-lepton curvature. The resulting 12 x 10 design matrix yields a second left-null relation, m_1 m_3 m_mu^2/(m_2^2 m_e m_tau) = 1. Using NuFIT 6.0 central splittings, M3 predicts m_lightest = 1.04 x 10^-4 eV and sum m_nu = 0.05889 eV for normal ordering, or m_lightest = 3.51 x 10^-3 eV and sum m_nu = 0.10269 eV for inverted ordering. M2 and M3 remain exploratory because they were selected after charged-sector diagnostics and the shared curvature has not been derived microscopically.

</details>


### [151] [Neutrino Fluxes at a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02714)
*Francis M. Burk,Tao Han,Wolfgang Kilian,Felix Kling,Joachim Kopp,Zahra Tabrizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了10 TeV缪子对撞机产生的超高能中微子通量，发现相互作用区附近的中微子通量比此前估计高出近两个数量级，并详细计算了由μ⁺μ⁻碰撞、电磁簇射及环内中微子相互作用产生的额外中微子源，指出这些通量在一吨级前向探测器中将产生可观的中微子事件数（包括多个tau中微子事件），并探讨了其在QCD与核物理、电弱精密测量及新物理搜寻方面的物理机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子对撞机虽主要被视为能量前沿的新物理精密探测装置，但其束流缪子衰变会产生大量高能中微子。以往对这部分中微子通量的估计可能存在明显不足，需要利用最新设计重新评估，以正确认识缪子对撞机作为中微子源的真实强度及其潜在物理价值。

Method: 基于最新设计的10 TeV缪子对撞机方案，重新计算了束流缪子在直线段（尤其实验周围）衰变产生的中微子通量；同时计算了μ⁺μ⁻碰撞产生的中微子、缪子衰变电子引发的电磁簇射产生的中微子，以及对撞环附近中微子相互作用产生的中微子三种额外源的贡献。

Result: 发现相互作用区附近的中微子通量较早期估计高出约两个数量级；额外中微子源不可忽略，在一吨级前向探测器中各味中微子相互作用事件数可观，其中包含多个tau中微子事件。

Conclusion: 缪子对撞机不仅是新物理精密探测平台，也是强有力的高能中微子源，其产生的多味中微子可用于QCD与核物理研究、电弱精密测量以及新物理搜寻等多种物理目标。

Abstract: While muon colliders are primarily considered precision machines for new physics searches at the energy frontier, they are also intense sources of high-energy neutrinos. Large fluxes of electron and muon neutrinos are produced in the decay of beam muons, especially along the straight sections around the experiments. In this work, we re-evaluate these neutrino fluxes using the latest design of a \SI{10}{TeV} muon collider and find that fluxes around the interaction region are almost two orders of magnitude higher than earlier estimates. We also compute the fluxes of neutrinos produced in $μ^+μ^-$ collisions, electromagnetic showers induced by electrons from muon decay, and interactions of neutrinos close to the collider ring. We find that these additional neutrino sources are non-negligible and would lead to a sizable number of neutrino interactions of all flavors, including several tau neutrino events, in a ton-scale detector placed in the forward direction. We discuss the physics opportunities offered by muon collider neutrinos in the context of QCD and nuclear physics, electroweak precision measurements, and searches for new physics.

</details>


### [152] [The Forward Neutrino Flux and its Secondaries at a 10 TeV Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02718)
*Ju-Yeol Choi,Matheus Hostert,Peiran Li,Zhen Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MINT，一个用于模拟10 TeV缪子对撞机中束流定向中微子通量的蒙特卡洛模拟工具。研究发现，距离对撞点5公里处的基准探测器每年可在约3吨的有效体积中探测到约10^9次中微子相互作用，束斑尺寸约为1米量级。文章还分析了岩石中中微子相互作用产生的次级缪子、中微子及来自粲介子和τ子产生的ν_τ背景，并讨论了利用该前向中微子暴露量搜索超越标准模型粒子的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子对撞机中缪子衰变会产生TeV量级的电子味和缪子味中微子束流。这些前所未有的高能中微子通量可用于基础物理研究，包括精确测量中微子性质、研究中微子-核子相互作用，以及搜索超越标准模型的新物理。然而，精确表征这些前向中微子通量（包括束流动力学的效应）需要一个专门的模拟工具，这正是MINT的设计初衷。

Method: 本文引入MINT（Muon collider INtense neutrino Telescope模拟），这是一个专用的蒙特卡洛模拟框架，用于在包含缪子束流动力学的情况下模拟中微子通量。研究者对10 TeV缪子对撞机的正/负缪子束流前向方向的中微子通量进行建模，计算了5公里处基准探测器的中微子相互作用率，并进一步分析了岩石中中微子相互作用产生的次级粒子（包括次级缪子和次级中微子，如粲介子衰变产生的ν_τ和错误味中微子）对探测器的污染。

Result: 研究发现：基准探测器每年可观测约10^9次中微子相互作用，束斑尺寸约1米。每次束团交叉约有两个高能、高度极化的次级缪子从岩石中穿过探测器。岩石中粲介子和τ子产生过程导致的次级ν_τ通量每年约0.2个事件，过小难以观测。错误味中微子（如μ^-束流中产生的ν_e+反ν_μ）数量更多，但仅为TeV中微子相互作用的约10^-9。文章还展示了这些前向中微子暴露量可用于搜索通过混合或电磁偶极子算符与电子/缪子味耦合的重中性轻子等超越标准模型粒子。

Conclusion: 10 TeV缪子对撞机的前向中微子通量提供了极高的中微子统计量（每年约10^9次相互作用），使其成为研究标准模型高能中微子物理和搜索新物理的独特平台。虽然岩石中产生的次级背景（如ν_τ和错误味中微子）相对较小，不构成严重干扰，但在实验设计中仍需加以考虑。该研究为未来缪子对撞机上的前向中微子探测器设计和新物理搜索潜力提供了重要的定量依据。

Abstract: Muon decays in a muon collider ring would produce TeV neutrino and antineutrino beams of electron and muon flavor. We study this flux in the forward $μ^+$ and $μ^-$ beam directions at a 10 TeV muon collider, introducing MINT, a dedicated Monte Carlo simulation to model neutrino fluxes including the muon beam dynamics. We find that a benchmark detector at 5 km from the interaction point would see about $\mathcal{O}(10^{9})$ neutrino interactions per year in a $\sim3$ tonne fiducial volume with a beam spot size of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ meter. We calculate the number of secondary muons and neutrinos generated by neutrino interactions in the rock upstream of the forward detectors and find that about two secondary high-energy and highly polarized muons from the rock would cross each detector per bunch crossing. Neutrino productions of charmed mesons and taus in the rock generate a small $ν_τ+\barν_τ$ secondary flux, with $\mathcal{O}(0.2)$ events per year in the detectors, likely too small to be observed. Wrong-sign neutrinos from similar processes, such as $ν_e+\barν_μ$ in the $μ^-$ beam, are more numerous but still of $\mathcal{O}(10^{-9})$ of the number of TeV neutrino interactions. Finally, we outline how the large forward neutrino exposure can be used to search for beyond-the-Standard-Model particles produced in neutrino interactions, with examples of heavy neutral leptons coupled to electron and muon flavors through mixing or electromagnetic dipole operators.

</details>


### [153] [Abundant production of scalars and axions from phase transition bubble expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02729)
*Isabel Garcia Garcia,Gaurang Ramakant Kane,John March-Russell,Andrea Paolini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了宇宙学一级相变中的粒子产生机制，发现真真空泡即使在恒定速度膨胀时也能高效产生粒子（当泡壁曲率半径超过粒子康普顿波长之前），并计算了加速和匀速两种膨胀史下的动量谱，证明该机制可参数性地主导其他微耦合粒子的产生过程（包括freeze-in），为暗辐射和暗物质提供了新来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知认为以恒定速度运动的泡壁不能辐射粒子，但作者发现考虑到泡壁的局部曲率半径时，即使匀速膨胀的真真空泡也能高效产生粒子。这填补了粒子宇宙学中关于一级相变粒子产生机制的一个重要空白，并为轻的微耦合粒子（如类轴子粒子）的产生提供了新的途径，可能对暗辐射和暗物质的解释有重要意义。

Method: 作者分析了自旋为0的大质量标量场与泡轮廓线性耦合的情况。通过分别考虑加速膨胀和恒定速度膨胀两种历史，计算了产生的粒子的动量谱，识别了相干和非相干产生的机制，并量化了微扰处理的适用性。此外，还将该机制应用于隐藏Yang-Mills扇区的一级退紧闭-紧闭相变中类轴子粒子的产生。

Result: 结果表明，即使泡壁以恒定径向速度膨胀，只要泡壁的局部静止系曲率半径小于粒子康普顿波长，就能高效产生粒子。该机制在某些参数区域可参数性地支配其他微耦合粒子的产生过程（包括freeze-in），并在隐藏Yang-Mills相变中能大量产生类轴子粒子，在广泛的参数空间中产生暗辐射和/或暗物质信号。

Conclusion: 这种基于泡壁曲率效应的粒子产生机制为轻的微耦合粒子提供了新的产生源，可应用于文献中研究的许多模型，为暗辐射和暗物质的起源提供了新的可能性，特别是在隐藏扇区的一级相变情景中具有重要现象学意义。

Abstract: We revisit aspects of particle production during cosmological first-order phase transitions, and show that the expansion of true-vacuum bubbles can be a copious source of particle production. Specifically, for a massive spin-0 field linearly coupled to the bubble profile, spherical bubbles expanding even at \emph{constant} radial velocity efficiently produce particles until the local-rest-frame radius of curvature of the bubble wall exceeds the particle Compton wavelength -- contrary to the expectation that walls moving at constant speed cannot radiate. We compute the momentum spectrum of the produced particles for both accelerated and constant-velocity expansion histories, identify the regimes of coherent and incoherent production, quantify the validity of the perturbative treatment, and show that this mechanism can parametrically dominate other production processes of feebly coupled particles, including freeze-in. Our results apply to many models of light, feebly coupled particles studied in the literature, providing new sources of dark radiation and dark matter. In particular, a first-order deconfinement-confinement transition in a hidden Yang-Mills sector can abundantly produce axion-like particles, leading to dark radiation and/or dark matter signatures over large regions of parameter space.

</details>


### [154] [High-Frequency Gravitational Wave Detection with Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02733)
*Heechan Yi,Seong Chan Park,Kyoungchul Kong,Myeonghun Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用超导transmon量子比特阵列（嵌入谐振微波腔）探测高频引力波（HFGW）的新型量子传感范式。通过逆Gertsenshtein效应，HFGW在静态磁场中激发出TE212腔模的电磁场，量子比特阵列以对称Dicke态纠缠配置，使信号概率随量子比特数平方增长，灵敏度标度达到h_min ∝ n_q^{-3/4}。800个理想化全局量子比特的灵敏度超越传统宏观腔功率极限五个数量级，有效克服引力相互作用的Planck尺度抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频引力波是探索高能和早期宇宙物理的独特窗口，但传统宏观干涉测量技术无法探测该频段。现有探测方案在GHz频段受限于Planck尺度引力相互作用的微弱抑制，亟需一种能够打破宏观腔功率极限的新型探测范式。

Method: 采用逆Gertsenshtein效应：HFGW在静态磁场中传播时共振激发腔模。利用TE212模电磁场的自旋-2四极模式，将超导transmon量子比特精确定位于电场热点作为局域传感器。将量子比特阵列配置为对称Dicke态纠缠量子寄存器，利用集体量子增强实现信号概率随量子比特数n_q的平方标度，从而获得h_min ∝ n_q^{-3/4}的应变灵敏度标度律。

Result: 理想化全局寄存器中800个量子比特可将应变灵敏度提升至超过传统宏观腔功率探测极限五个数量级。以代表性axion-haloscope参数为基准进行评估，该集体量子增强方案有效缓解了引力相互作用固有的Planck尺度信号抑制问题。

Conclusion: 该量子传感框架为GHz频段下一代高频引力波搜索建立了变革性探测范式，通过量子集体纠缠增强克服了传统探测的灵敏度瓶颈，开辟了基于量子比特阵列的高频引力波探测新途径。

Abstract: High-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) provide a unique window into high-energy and early-universe physics, yet they evade traditional macroscopic interferometry. To bridge this detection gap, we propose a novel quantum-sensing paradigm utilizing superconducting transmon qubits embedded in resonant microwave cavities. Through the inverse Gertsenshtein effect, HFGWs propagating in a static magnetic field resonantly excite a cavity mode. By leveraging the characteristic spin-2 quadrupolar pattern of the induced electromagnetic field, we position qubits directly at the electric-field hot spots of the $\mathrm{TE}_{212}$ mode to act as localized sensors. Crucially, configuring this array as an entangled quantum register via symmetric Dicke states unlocks a fundamental scaling advantage: the signal probability scales quadratically with the qubit number, translating to a $h_{\min} \propto n_q^{-3/4}$ strain sensitivity scaling. We demonstrate that an idealized global register of 800 qubits reaches a strain sensitivity that surpasses standard macroscopic cavity-power limits by five orders of magnitude. Benchmarked against representative axion-haloscope parameters, this collective quantum enhancement decisively mitigates the profound Planck-scale suppression inherent to gravitational interactions, establishing a transformative framework for next-generation HFGW searches in the GHz band.

</details>


### [155] [Virtues and Vices of Equivariant Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02735)
*Luigi Favaro,Tilman Plehn,Huilin Qu,Jonas Spinner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了Lorentz等变Transformer在大尺寸喷注标记和味标记中的优势，通过优化推理成本指标来控制计算需求。缩放研究表明，在几何特征相关的情况下，Lorentz等变网络优于标准Transformer，这为LHC数据的基座模型开发提供了有价值的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC（大型强子对撞机）数据中的喷注标记和味标记是粒子物理中重要的分类任务。标准Transformer虽然在各种任务中表现出色，但缺乏对Lorentz对称性（狭义相对论中的时空对称性）的显式编码。作者希望首次探索Lorentz等变Transformer在这些任务中的潜在优势，并考虑实际推理成本这一关键现实约束。

Method: 作者设计并实现了Lorentz等变的Transformer架构，并针对推理成本指标（如计算量、延迟等）对所有实现进行了优化。通过系统的缩放研究（scaling studies），在理想资源条件（充足计算资源）和资源受限两种场景下，将Lorentz等变网络与标准Transformer进行对比评估。

Result: 研究发现，只要几何特征（即与Lorentz对称性相关的物理量）在任务中具有相关性，Lorentz等变网络在两种场景下都优于标准Transformer。此外，Lorentz等变性带来的条件增益为LHC数据基座模型（foundation models）的开发提供了有价值的输入和参考。

Conclusion: Lorentz等变Transformer在大尺寸喷注标记和味标记任务中展现出优于标准Transformer的性能，前提是几何特征具有相关性。这一发现对计算资源受限的实际场景尤为重要，并为LHC数据基座模型的架构设计提供了指导方向。

Abstract: We study for the first time the benefit of Lorentz-equivariant transformers for large-size jet tagging and flavor tagging. To control their computing demands, we optimize all implementations for inference cost metrics. In our scaling studies, we find that Lorentz-equivariant networks outperform standard transformers, provided geometric features are relevant. This holds true in an idealized world as well as for limited resources. The conditional gain from Lorentz equivariance provides interesting input to the development of foundation models for LHC data.

</details>


### [156] [Flavor-violating dark matter at MEG-II and Mu3e](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02740)
*Daniele Barducci,Giulio Marino,Paolo Panci,Robert Ziegler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高强μ子实验（MEG II和Mu3e）通过轻子味破坏衰变μ⁺→e⁺χχ̄探测暗物质的潜力，发现精密Michel谱测量可探测TeV量级的新物理，并通过辐射衰变通道补充了轻暗物质的探测窗口，还能探测与freeze-in机制相关的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质直接探测和碰撞机搜索存在局限性，需要寻找新的互补探测途径。轻子味破坏（LFV）过程对TeV尺度新物理高度敏感，而高强μ子实验为探索暗物质与轻子间的相互作用提供了独特机会。

Method: 采用维度六四费米子算符统一描述μ⁺→e⁺χχ̄衰变中的底层相互作用，系统遍历所有允许的Lorentz结构；分别分析非辐射衰变（通过Michel谱精密测量）和辐射衰变μ⁺→e⁺χχ̄γ（在MEG II实验中探测）两个互补通道的灵敏度。

Result: 发现Michel谱的精密测量可对~1 MeV以上暗物质质量探测TeV量级的新物理标度；对更轻的暗物质，MEG II的辐射衰变通道提供补充灵敏度，部分参数空间甚至优于非辐射通道。此外，在特定再加热温度区间（几十MeV以下但高于BBN约束），MEG II和Mu3e可探测freeze-in机制产生观测暗物质丰度的参数区域。

Conclusion: 高强μ子实验通过LFV衰变通道为暗物质探测提供了与现有方法互补的有效途径，尤其在轻暗物质区域和freeze-in产生机制方面展现了独特优势。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of high-intensity muon experiments such as MEG~II and Mu3e to uncover dark matter (DM) through the lepton-flavor-violating decay $μ^+\to e^+χ\barχ$. We describe the underlying interactions in terms of dimension-six four-fermion operators and systematically explore all allowed Lorentz structures. We show that precision measurements of the Michel spectrum can probe new-physics scales of ${\cal O}({\rm TeV})$ for DM masses above approximately $1~{\rm MeV}$. For lighter DM, whose signal is confined close to the Michel endpoint, the radiative decay $μ^+\to e^+χ\barχγ$ at MEG~II opens a complementary window, with sensitivity comparable to and, in some regions, stronger than that of the non-radiative channel. Remarkably, for reheating temperatures below the tens-of-MeV scale but above the BBN bound, MEG~II and Mu3e can probe regions in which freeze-in through the very same LFV interactions accounts for the observed DM abundance.

</details>


### [157] [Fermionic Dark Matter in a Scotogenic Model with a Complex Scalar Singlet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02794)
*Gustavo Ardila-Tafurth,Andrés Flórez,Victor Martín-Lozano,Avelino Vicente*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了复标量单态（complex scoto-singlet）模型的现象学，该模型是scotogenic框架的扩展，同时解释了辐射性中微子质量和费米子暗物质。通过MCMC扫描参数空间并结合理论一致性及实验约束，发现可行的暗物质解存在于希格斯共振区和共湮灭区，并扩展了最小scotogenic模型所允许的质量范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为同时解释中微子质量与暗物质，作者提出scotogenic框架的扩展模型（complex scoto-singlet model），并希望通过系统扫描参数空间来全面研究其可观测现象学，评估其在直接探测、间接探测和对撞机实验中的可检验性。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法扫描模型参数空间，施加理论自洽性约束，并结合中微子振荡数据、轻子味破坏（lepton-flavor violation）、对撞机搜索以及暗物质遗迹丰度观测等多项实验约束进行综合分析。

Result: 发现可行的暗物质解主要出现在希格斯共振区和共湮灭区域，相比最小scotogenic模型扩展了允许的暗物质质量范围。直接探测和间接探测信号高度抑制，大部分参数空间超出当前实验灵敏度。在对撞机上，压缩谱场景中单喷注（monojet）搜索最具探测前景，而长寿命带电标量可能产生位移顶点或探测器稳定信号。

Conclusion: complex scoto-singlet模型为辐射性中微子质量和费米子暗物质提供了统一的框架，其中暗物质的探测主要依赖对撞机上的单喷注搜索和长寿命带电标量信号，而直接和间接探测实验的灵敏度有限。

Abstract: We study the phenomenology of the complex scoto-singlet model, an extension of the scotogenic framework that simultaneously accounts for radiative neutrino masses and fermionic dark matter. We perform a Markov Chain Monte Carlo scan of the parameter space, imposing theoretical consistency together with constraints from neutrino oscillation data, lepton-flavor violation, collider searches and the observed dark matter relic abundance. Viable dark matter solutions are found both near the Higgs resonance and in coannihilation regions, extending the allowed mass range with respect to the minimal scotogenic model. Direct- and indirect-detection signals are strongly suppressed, placing most of the viable parameter space beyond the reach of current experiments. At colliders, monojet searches offer the best prospects to probe our model in compressed-spectrum scenarios, while long-lived charged scalars may give rise to displaced or detector-stable signatures.

</details>


### [158] [Critical coupling with zero-mode corrections in discretized light-cone quantized $φ^4$ theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02972)
*Shreeram Jawadekar,Mamoon A. Sharaf,James P. Vary*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在二维φ⁴理论中求解离散光锥量子化（DLCQ）哈密顿量质量谱的改进方法，该方法纳入微扰零模贡献，加速了质量本征态随分辨率增加的数值收敛，并在基空间维度减少一个数量级以上时获得临界耦合23.10±0.25（与不含零模修正的较大基空间结果23.53±0.26相当），使用高斯过程回归外推连续极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLCQ方法在求解二维φ⁴理论质量谱时，需要非常大的基空间才能获得足够精度的结果，计算成本高昂。零模贡献在DLCQ中通常被忽略或处理不够精确，若能纳入零模的微扰修正，有望显著提升收敛速度并降低计算开销，使该方法扩展到更高维规范理论成为可能。

Method: 作者在DLCQ框架中引入微扰零模贡献，将其纳入哈密顿量的求解过程。通过这种修正，系统的数值收敛速度随分辨率（基空间大小）增加而显著加快。在临界耦合的连续极限外推中，采用高斯过程回归（GPR）方法替代传统的多项式外推，以获得更鲁棒的外推估计。

Result: 在基空间维度减少一个数量级以上的条件下，外推得到的临界耦合为23.10±0.25，与使用更大基空间但不含零模修正的结果23.53±0.26在误差范围内一致，但计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 纳入微扰零模贡献的DLCQ方法能显著提升数值效率，以更小的基空间获得可比精度的质量谱和临界耦合结果。该方法未来有望推广应用于更高维规范理论的研究。

Abstract: We present an advancement for solving the Discretized Light-Cone Quantization (DLCQ) Hamiltonian for mass spectra in 2D $φ^4$ theory that incorporates perturbative zero-mode contributions. We demonstrate that this correction accelerates numerical convergence of the mass eigenstates with increasing resolution and yields a critical coupling of comparable accuracy with a substantial reduction in computational costs. Specifically, with more than one order of magnitude reduction in basis space dimensionality we achieve an extrapolated critical coupling of $23.10 \pm 0.25$ compared with $23.53 \pm 0.26$ in the larger basis but without zero-mode correction. We employ Gaussian Process Regression for extrapolation to the continuum limit. The approach we present here is prospective for studies of higher-dimensional gauge theories.

</details>


### [159] [Neutrino flavor-wave transport: Numerical tests and theoretical challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02984)
*Anson Kost,Lucas Johns,Huaiyu Duan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次数值计算了中微子味波（flavomon）输运在慢驱动条件下的行为，发现非线性波-波耦合和由简并与小能隙引起的非绝热跃迁是两大关键理论挑战，需要进一步解决才能使该方法在核坍缩超新星和中子星并合等天体物理场景中可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心坍缩超新星和中子星并合等中微子稠密环境中的中微子量子动力学计算上难以处理。味波（或flavomon）输运作为一种新兴方法，将小尺度味不均匀性视为由局部平均背景决定的准粒子来处理，有望降低计算复杂度。本文旨在首次检验该方法在慢驱动条件下的可行性。

Method: 采用味波输运框架，将小尺度味不均匀性作为准粒子（flavomon）处理，进行首次慢驱动条件下的数值计算。同时考察了味波平行输运和味空间对称性破缺等额外问题。

Result: 数值结果显示定量上有部分成功，但暴露了两个关键问题：(1) 非线性波-波耦合可能很重要；(2) 简并态和小能隙导致显著的非绝热跃迁。

Conclusion: 味波输运方法虽展现一定潜力，但必须解决非线性波-波耦合和非绝热跃迁两大挑战，才能在天体物理相关场景中成为可行的计算方法。

Abstract: Neutrino quantum kinetics is computationally intractable in the neutrino-dense arenas of core-collapse supernovae and neutron star mergers. Flavor-wave (or flavomon) transport is an emerging approach to this problem in which small-scale flavor inhomogeneities are treated as quasiparticles with properties determined by the local mean background. We present the first numerical calculations of flavor-wave transport under slow driving. Our results show some quantitative successes but also underscore the theoretical challenges that need to be overcome. Two issues are particularly concerning. (1) Nonlinear wave-wave coupling may be important. (2) Degeneracies and small energy gaps are responsible for significant nonadiabatic transitions. Future work will need to address these points if flavor-wave transport is to be viable in settings of astrophysical interest. Other topics examined include flavor-wave parallel transport and flavor-space symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [160] [Minimal Scale-Invariant Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03019)
*Alexander Belyaev,Roman Pasechnik,Rishav Roshan,Alfonso Zerwekh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含单个Z2稳定实标量单态的、最小类尺度不变SM扩展模型。该模型的单态质量不是独立输入参数，而是通过量子有效势动态产生，并通过Higgs portal与辐射性电弱对称破缺相关联。作者构建了双场单圈有效势，引入修正的on-shell重整化方案，排除了热冻结机制后识别出高预测性的冻结（freeze-in）实现，在微扰性、真空稳定性和观测遗迹密度约束下得到约2 MeV的暗物质质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有最小自由度的尺度不变SM扩展模型，该模型可以同时动态解释单态暗物质质量的起源和电弱对称破缺，为暗物质产生机制提供与辐射性电弱对称破缺直接关联的高预测性框架。

Method: 构造双场单圈有效势，引入修正的on-shell重整化方案固定在物理点处的电弱真空、Higgs质量、Higgs-单态混合消失以及单态曲率为零。基于直接探测限制排除热冻结机制后，采用冻结（freeze-in）产生机制，并施加微扰性、真空稳定性和遗迹丰度约束来确定模型参数。

Result: 识别出满足所有约束的冻结方案，预测暗物质质量约为2 MeV。该解对应的电子散射截面远低于DAMIC-M当前的灵敏度，因此现有直接探测实验无法约束该场景。

Conclusion: 最小尺度不变单态模型提供了一个稳健且高预测性的框架，将辐射性电弱对称破缺与冻结暗物质产生机制联系起来，且不受当前直接探测实验的约束。

Abstract: We study the minimal classically scale-invariant extension of the Standard Model, containing a single $\mathbb Z_2$-stabilised real scalar singlet whose mass is not an independent input but is generated dynamically through the quantum effective potential and linked to radiative electroweak symmetry breaking through the Higgs portal. We construct the full two-field one-loop effective potential and introduce a modified on-shell renormalisation scheme that fixes the electroweak vacuum, the Higgs mass, the vanishing Higgs--singlet mixing and the singlet curvature at the physical point, yielding predictions stable under renormalisation-scale variation. Since thermal freeze-out is excluded by direct-detection limits, we identify a highly predictive freeze-in realisation of this model. Imposing perturbativity, vacuum stability and the observed relic abundance leads to a freeze-in solution with a dark-matter mass of around $2~{\rm MeV}$. The predicted electron-scattering cross section for this solution lies far below the current sensitivity of DAMIC-M. Current direct-detection experiments therefore do not constrain this scenario. The minimal scale-invariant singlet model thus provides a robust and highly predictive framework connecting radiative electroweak symmetry breaking and freeze-in dark-matter genesis.

</details>


### [161] [Possible Bound States in the $D^\ast\bar D^\ast$/$B^\ast\bar B^\ast$ and $D^\ast D^\ast$/$\bar B^\ast\bar B^\ast$ Systems within the Bethe-Salpeter Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03104)
*Ce Li,Jing-Juan Qi,Zhu-Feng Zhang,Zhen-Yang Wang,Xin-Heng Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Bethe-Salpeter形式和单玻色子交换相互作用，研究了D*D̄*、B*B̄*、D*D*和B̄*B̄*系统中的S波束缚态，发现隐藏重味系统在J^PC=0^{++}、1^{+-}和2^{++}下存在同位旋标量束缚态，双重子系统在特定I(J^P)组态下存在束缚态，且底夸克系统比粲夸克系统更容易形成束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重夸克偶素及双重子系统（如D*D̄*、B*B̄*等）中是否可能存在S波束缚态，这些态可能对应实验上观测到的XYZ类奇特强子态，有助于理解量子色动力学在低能区的非微扰行为。

Method: 采用Bethe-Salpeter形式框架，利用单玻色子交换势作为相互作用核，系统求解D*D̄*、B*B̄*、D*D*和B̄*B̄*四类系统的S波束缚态方程。

Result: 在隐藏重味系统中，同位旋标量情况下J^{PC}=0^{++}、1^{+-}和2^{++}存在束缚态解，但同位旋矢量情况下在所研究参数范围内无解。在双重子系统中，I(J^P)=0(1^+)、1(0^+)和1(2^+)存在束缚态解，其中1(0^+)需要较大的截断参数。底系统因约化质量更大而比粲系统更容易形成束缚态。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，隐藏重味和双重子系统在特定量子数下均可形成S波束缚态，底介子分子态比粲介子对应态更稳定，为实验上寻找相关奇特强子态提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate possible $S$-wave bound states in the $D^\ast\bar D^\ast$, $B^\ast\bar B^\ast$, $D^\ast D^\ast$, and $\bar B^\ast\bar B^\ast$ systems within the Bethe-Salpeter formalism using one-boson-exchange interactions. Bound state solutions are obtained in the isoscalar hidden-heavy systems with $J^{PC}=0^{++}$, $1^{+-}$, and $2^{++}$, whereas no isovector solutions are found within the parameter range considered. For the doubly heavy systems, solutions are obtained in the allowed $I(J^P)=0(1^+)$, $1(0^+)$, and $1(2^+)$ systems, although the $1(0^+)$ solution requires a comparatively large cutoff parameter. The bottom systems are bounded more favorably than their charmed counterparts because of their larger reduced masses.

</details>


### [162] [Spin correlation of hyperon-antihyperon systems in $e^+e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03131)
*Wenyu Zhang,Yang Li,Xiaorong Zhou,Qun Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在BESIII实验的e⁺e⁻→B\bar{B}过程中，计算了自旋1/2粒子-反粒子系统（Λ\barΛ、Σ⁺\barΣ⁻、Λ_c⁺\barΛ_c⁻）的量子信息可观测量（Bell变量、并发度、负性），发现超子-反超子系统中的横向极化显著限制了CHSH不等式的违背区域但对纠缠影响不大，并推广到一般自旋1/2粒子-反粒子产生情形，探索了量子关联作为强子结构（特别是重味介子内部部分子纠缠）探针的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高能碰撞中的量子关联作为研究基础物理和强子结构的新视角。超子-反超子系统展现出不同于基本粒子-反粒子系统的特性（非零横向极化），需要理解这些效应对量子信息可观测量（如Bell不等式的违背）的影响，并挖掘量子关联作为部分子层面纠缠探针的潜力。

Method: 从两量子比特密度算符出发，计算Bell变量、并发度（concurrence）和负性（negativity）作为散射角和碰撞能量的函数，针对e⁺e⁻→B\bar{B}过程（B=Λ、Σ⁺、Λ_c⁺）中三个超子-反超子系统，在BESIII实验框架下进行数值分析。同时将分析推广到一般自旋1/2粒子-反粒子产生情形，并延伸至部分子层面的纠缠研究。

Result: 发现与基本粒子-反粒子系统不同，超子-反超子系统在产生平面外存在非零横向极化，这显著限制了CHSH不等式被违背的动力学区域（即Bell非局域性成立的范围），但纠缠（以并发度和负性度量）基本不受影响。此外，扩展分析展示了利用量子关联探测强子结构、特别是重味介子内部部分子纠缠的可能性。

Conclusion: 超子-反超子系统的横向极化会缩小CHSH不等式违背的观测窗口，这是区分超子系统中Bell非局域性与纠缠检测的重要物理效应；量子关联可作为一种新的探针用于研究强子内部的部分子级纠缠，为高能对撞实验中量子信息与强子结构研究提供交叉方向。

Abstract: Quantum correlations in high-energy collisions provide a novel perspective on both fundamental physics and hadron structure. We calculate the quantum information observable in spin-1/2 particle-antiparticle systems produced in $e^+e^-$ annihilation. Starting from the two-qubit density operator, we calculate the Bell variable, concurrence and negativity as functions of the scattering angle and collision energy for the $Λ\barΛ$, $Σ^+\barΣ^-$, and $Λ^+_c\barΛ^-_c$ systems in the process $e^+e^-\rightarrow B\bar{B}$ at BESIII experiments. Unlike elementary particle-antiparticle systems, the hyperon-antihyperon system exhibits non-vanishing transverse polarization for $B$ and $\bar{B}$ with respect to the production plane, which significantly restrict the kinematic region where the CHSH inequality is violated while leaving the entanglement largely unaffected. We also extend our analysis to the general case of spin-1/2 particle-antiparticle production, and explore the potential of using quantum correlations as probes to hadron structure, particularly to the parton-level entanglement inside heavy-flavored mesons.

</details>


### [163] [Faddeev equations for the $J/ψ\,NN$ and $φ\,NN$ three-body systems in momentum space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03162)
*Xu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在动量空间中构建了J/ψ NN和φ NN三体系统的Faddeev方程，利用HAL QCD相互作用势和Malfliet-Tjon势求解两体子系统t矩阵，数值结果显示φ NN三体系统中存在束缚态，而J/ψ NN系统中不存在束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重夸克偶素（J/ψ）和奇异介子（φ）与两个核子构成的三体系统中是否存在束缚态，从而加深对重味介子与核子相互作用的理解。

Method: 在动量空间中构建J/ψ NN和φ NN三体系统的Faddeev方程；通过求解Lippmann-Schwinger方程获得两体子系统t矩阵；NN相互作用采用Malfliet-Tjon势；J/ψ N在4S3/2和2S1/2道及φ N在4S3/2道的相互作用势取自HAL QCD；2S1/2道φN相互作用由φp关联函数分析和HAL QCD结果组合得到，并假设φN与J/ψN相互作用的自旋-自旋部分与各自强子质量成反比。

Result: 数值结果表明φ NN三体系统中存在束缚态，而J/ψ NN系统中不存在束缚态。

Conclusion: φ介子可能以三体重子系统（φ NN）的束缚态形式存在，而J/ψ NN系统则没有形成束缚态，这一结果对理解重味介子在核物质中的行为具有参考意义。

Abstract: We construct Faddeev equations for the $J/ψ\,NN$ and $φ\,NN$ three-body systems in momentum space. The two-body subsystem $t$-matrix is obtained by solving the Lippmann-Schwinger equations. The $NN$ interactions are constructed using the Malfliet-Tjon potential. The $J/ψ\,N$ interaction potentials in the ${}^4S_{3/2}$ and ${}^2S_{1/2}$ channels are taken from HAL QCD. The $φ\,N$ interaction potential in the ${}^4S_{3/2}$ channel is also taken from HAL QCD. The ${}^2S_{1/2}$ $φN$ interaction is obtained from the combination of the $φp$ correlation function analysis and the HAL QCD results, as well as from the assumption that the spin-spin parts of the $φN$ and $J/ψN$ interactions are inversely proportional to the respective hadron masses. The Faddeev equations are solved to find bound states in the $J/ψ\,NN$ and $φ\,NN$ three-body systems. The numerical results suggest that there exist bound states in the $φ\,NN$ three-body system, while there is no bound state in the $J/ψ\,NN$ system.

</details>


### [164] [Unraveling weak radiative hyperon decays with broken flavor symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03248)
*Kaiwen Chen,Lin Qiu,Jin Sun,Zhi-Peng Xing,Ruilin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对自旋1/2超子的弱辐射衰变进行了破缺的SU(3)味对称性分析，纳入流-流和电磁企鹅贡献以及电荷和质量插入效应。通过六个独立约化振幅和七个自由参数，全局拟合十个可观测物理量得到χ²/d.o.f.=0.98，成功容纳Σ⁺→pγ的大负不对称性，并预测Ξ⁻→Σ⁻γ的衰变不对称性与当前实验结果存在约1.3σ的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超子弱辐射衰变是研究味对称性破缺和CP破坏的重要探针，但此前理论计算与实验观测（特别是Σ⁺→pγ的大负不对称性）之间存在显著矛盾，需要更完整的对称性分析框架来解释实验数据。

Method: 采用破缺的SU(3)味对称性分析，包含流-流贡献、电磁企鹅贡献以及电荷和质量插入效应；建立了六个独立的约化振幅来描述所有衰变道，并通过宇称守恒与宇称破坏形状因子之间的最小关系将自由参数压缩到八个；对十个可用观测量进行全局拟合。

Result: 全局拟合得到χ²/d.o.f.=0.98，成功容纳Σ⁺→pγ的大负不对称性；得到非零的有效形状因子g_{b8}，为宇称破坏振幅提供了味对称性实现，同时与Hara定理保持一致；预测α_γ(Ξ⁻→Σ⁻γ)为较大的负值，与当前实验值相差约1.3σ。

Conclusion: 破缺的SU(3)对称性分析能够同时解释现有超子弱辐射衰变数据，且g_{b8}的非零值为Hara定理与实验观测之间的张力提供了调和方案。Ξ⁻→Σ⁻γ的精确测量将成为检验该理论预言的决定性实验。

Abstract: We present a broken $SU(3)$ flavor analysis of weak radiative decays of spin-1/2 hyperons, incorporating current-current and electromagnetic-penguin contributions as well as charge- and mass-insertion effects. Six independent reduced amplitudes describe all decay channels, while a minimal relation between the parity-conserving and parity-violating form factors reduces to eight free parameters. A global fit to the ten available observables yields $χ^2/\mathrm{d.o.f.}=0.98$ and accommodates the large negative asymmetry in $Σ^+\to pγ$. The resulting nonzero effective form factor $g_{b_8}$ provides a possible flavor-symmetry realization of the parity-violating amplitude while remaining compatible with Hara's theorem, which constrains the current-current contribution in the exact symmetry limit. Our analysis favors a sizable negative value of $α_γ(Ξ^- \to Σ^- γ)$, which differs from the current experimental result by approximately $1.3σ$. Future precision measurements of this observable will provide a decisive test of this prediction.

</details>


### [165] [Analysis of the $X(2370)$ as a glueball based on rigorous current-field duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03362)
*Zhi-Gang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将X(2370)（J^PC=0^-+）视为由三个价胶子构成的胶球，基于严格的流-场对偶性构造六夸克流，获得胶球-夸克拉格朗日量，并利用Fierz变换将夸克流玻色化为一系列三个赝标介子，最终以模型无关的方式得到胶球衰变到三个赝标介子的部分衰变宽度之比，结果与BESIII实验数据一致，支持将X(2370)认定为胶球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确认X(2370)粒子是否可被归为胶球（由胶子组成的束缚态），需要通过理论上模型无关的计算来检验其衰变性质是否与实验观测相符。

Method: 将X(2370)视作由三个价胶子构成的0^-+胶球；基于流-场对偶性构造六夸克流以获得胶球-夸克拉格朗日量；随后通过Fierz变换将夸克流玻色化为三个赝标介子的组合，从而模型无关地计算部分衰变宽度之比。

Result: 计算得到的胶球衰变到三个赝标介子的部分衰变宽度之比与BESIII合作组的实验数据吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将X(2370)认定为胶球，即由纯胶子自由度构成的新型强子态。

Abstract: In this work, we take the $X(2370)$ with $J^{PC}=0^{-+}$ as a glueball consists of three valence gluons, and construct a six-quark current based on rigorous current-field duality to obtain the glueball-quark Lagrangian. Then we perform Fierz transformation to bosonize the quark current into a series of three pseudoscalar mesons. At last, we obtain ratios among the partial decay widths of the glueball to three pseudoscalar mesons in a model-independent way, which are compatible with the experimental data from the BESIII Collaboration and support assigning the $X(2370)$ as a glueball.

</details>


### [166] [On the nature of fully-charmed four-quark exotic state $X(6900)$ from its photoproduction off nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03374)
*E. Ya. Paryev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在近运动学阈值条件下，通过原子核上的包含性光生过程来探测全粲四夸克态X(6900)的内部结构。基于核谱函数的碰撞模型考虑了三种内部结构情景（紧致四夸克、J/ψ-ψ(3770)分子态及二者混合），计算了碳-12和钨-184靶核上的激发函数、动量微分截面和透明度比等可观测量，发现这些量对X(6900)的内在结构具有明确敏感性，可为未来中美电子-离子对撞机实验提供结构判定的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X(6900)作为首个观测到的全粲四夸克态候选者，其内部结构（紧致四夸克、隐粲介子分子态或混合态）尚不明确。确定其内在结构对于理解QCD中多夸克态的性质至关重要，而生成本的研究动机即在于探索一种能够区分不同内部结构的实验途径。

Method: 采用基于核谱函数的碰撞模型，在30-40 GeV近阈值光子能量区间内，对12C和184W靶核上的X(6900)光生过程进行理论计算。模型同时考虑直生光子-核子相互作用中的X(6900)产生，并分别针对紧致四夸克、J/ψ-ψ(3770)分子态和两者混合三种内部结构情景，计算绝对和相对激发函数、动量微分截面及其比值，以及35 GeV光子能量下的透明度比A依赖性。

Result: 计算结果表明，所涉及的各类可观测量（激发函数、动量微分截面比值、透明度比）对X(6900)的内部结构展现出明确的敏感度，即不同内部结构假设会导致可区分的理论预言。

Conclusion: 近阈值的核靶光生实验可以作为区分X(6900)内部结构的有效探针。将此理论预言与未来美国及中国电子-离子对撞机上的实验数据进行比较，有望确定X(6900)的真正内在结构。

Abstract: The possibility to study the nature of fully-charmed tetraquark state $X(6900)$ from its inclusive photoproduction off nuclei near the kinematic threshold is investigated within the collision model based on the nuclear spectral function. The model accounts for $X(6900)$ production in direct photon--nucleon interactions as well as three different scenarios for its internal structure: compact tetraquark, molecule of the two hidden-charm $J/ψ$ and $ψ(3770)$ mesons and the mixture of both of them. We calculate within these scenarios the absolute and relative excitation functions on $^{12}$C and $^{184}$W nuclei at near-threshold photon energies of 30--40 GeV, the absolute momentum differential cross sections and ratios of them for the $X(6900)$ meson production off these target nuclei at laboratory polar angles of 0$^{\circ}$--5$^{\circ}$ and for photon energy of 35 GeV as well as the A-dependences of its transparency ratios at photon energy of 35 GeV. We show that these observables reveal a definite sensitivity to the $X(6900)$ intrinsic structure. Therefore, they might be useful for the determination of this structure from the comparison of them with the experimental data from the future experiments at the upcoming experimental facilities, such as the planned electron-ion colliders in the United States and China.

</details>


### [167] [Probing Heavy-Quark Spin Symmetry in Double $J/ψ$ Hadroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03510)
*Zhi-Guo He,Xiao-Bo Jin,Bernd A. Kniehl*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架下，对高横动量J/ψ对的强子产生进行了完整的O(α_s^5)分析，包含了所有可能的福克态组合。通过CMS和ATLAS数据约束了特定的LDME线性组合，结合单J/ψ产生数据确定了新的LDME组，该组与世界数据兼容，并验证了重夸克自旋对称性，成功预测了LHCb的η_c产生数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: J/ψ对产生是研究NRQCD因子化框架和长距离矩阵元（LDMEs）的重要探针。之前对J/ψ对产生的QCD修正计算不完整，特别是缺乏对所有Fock态组合的完整O(α_s^5)分析。同时，已有的LDME组存在自洽性和兼容性问题，需要通过更多独立可观测量来约束和验证。

Method: 在NRQCD因子化框架下，对J/ψ对强子产生进行完整的O(α_s^5)微扰QCD计算，包含所有福克态组合c\bar{c}(m)+c\bar{c}(n)，其中m,n取^3S_1^[1,8]、^1S_0^[8]、^3P_J^[1,8]。利用CMS和ATLAS的实验数据约束LDME组合，并结合单J/ψ产生的两个独立组合，构造新的LDME集合。进一步通过重夸克自旋对称性联系不同自旋态的LDME，检验η_c产生预言。

Result: CMS和ATLAS数据约束了LDMEs ⟨O^{J/ψ}(^1S_0^[8])⟩与⟨O^{J/ψ}(^3P_0^[8])⟩的特定线性组合。结合单J/ψ产生的约束，得到新的LDME组，与世界各地的prompt J/ψ产额和极化数据高度兼容。该LDME组满足重夸克自旋对称性，相应预言与LHCb的prompt η_c产额数据一致。O(α_s^5)的预言在J/ψ对不变质量的低端区域（超出领头阶运动学范围）与CMS和ATLAS实验数据吻合良好。

Conclusion: 完整的高阶QCD计算表明：J/ψ对产生数据能够提供对LDMEs的新约束，得到的LDME组不仅自洽地描述了单J/ψ和J/ψ对的产生数据，还满足重夸克自旋对称性并能预言η_c产生。这验证了NRQCD因子化框架在重夸克偶素产生研究中的有效性。O(α_s^5)的贡献在低不变质量区间起关键作用，显著改善了与实验的符合程度。

Abstract: We perform the first complete $\mathcal{O}(α_s^5)$ analysis of the prompt hadroproduction of $J/ψ$ pairs with large transverse momenta $p_T^{ψψ}$ in the nonrelativistic-QCD factorization framework, including all possible Fock state combinations $c\bar{c}(m)+c\bar{c}(n)$, with $m,n={}^3S_1^{[1,8]},{}^1S_0^{[8]},{}^3P_J^{[1,8]}$. We observe that CMS and ATLAS data constrain a specific linear combination of the long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) $\langle\mathcal{O}^{J/ψ}({}^1S_0^{[8]})\rangle$ and $\langle\mathcal{O}^{J/ψ}({}^3P_0^{[8]})\rangle$. In conjunction with two other combinations fixed by single prompt $J/ψ$ hadroproduction, we gain a new LDME set, which turns out to be largely compatible with the world data of prompt $J/ψ$ yield and polarization and to probe heavy-quark spin symmetry, by which agreement is established with LHCb data of prompt $η_c$ yield. Our $\mathcal{O}(α_s^5)$ predictions also nicely agree with CMS and ATLAS data in the lowest bins of $J/ψ$ pair invariant mass $m^{ψψ}$, beyond leading-order kinematics.

</details>


### [168] [Hierarchical Axiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03678)
*Hai-Jun Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"层级轴子宇宙"（hierarchical axiverse）概念：弦论紧致化产生的O(100)个轻轴子，在低能混合约束下呈现出层级排列的质量谱和分裂分布的衰变常数谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦论紧致化通常产生约100个轻轴子粒子（axion-like particles），但此前低能混合从未被用作其谱结构的约束条件。作者旨在利用低能混合作为结构化约束，揭示轴子谱的隐藏规律。

Method: 作者考虑两类混合效应：（1）QCD诱导的质量混合，要求轴子质量呈现层级间隔的有序排列；（2）随机混合效应，导致衰变常数呈现层级分裂分布——即两个由近空窗口隔开的人群。作者还验证了这些模式对紫外（UV）输入不敏感，并满足标准大体积紧致化中的Swampland约束。

Result: 发现两个普适模式：质量层级有序排列（由QCD混合驱动）和衰变常数的双人群分裂分布（由随机混合驱动）。两模式均不依赖紫外细节，且在标准紧致化框架内满足Swampland约束。

Conclusion: 上述层级模式被命名为"层级轴子宇宙"，为弦论轴子谱提供了一种低能约束下的系统性结构特征，可作为未来轴子搜索的预测性框架。

Abstract: String compactifications generically produce ${\cal O}(100)$ light axion-like particles, yet low-energy mixing has not previously been exploited as a structuring constraint on their spectrum. We show that the requirement of well-defined QCD-induced mass mixing forces the axion masses into a hierarchically spaced ordering, while stochastic mixing organizes the decay constants into a hierarchically split distribution -- two populations separated by an essentially empty window. Both patterns are insensitive to ultraviolet input and satisfy the relevant Swampland constraints within standard large-volume compactifications. We term this emergent pattern the hierarchical axiverse.

</details>


### [169] [Two-loop QCD amplitudes for $t\bar{t}W$ production at the LHC in the leading-colour approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03746)
*Matteo Becchetti,Dhimiter Canko,Xiang Chen,Vsevolod Chestnov,Maximilian Delto,Sara Ditsch,Tiziano Peraro,Mattia Pozzoli,Chiara Savoini,Simone Zoia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在广义领头色近似下，LHC中伴随W玻色子的顶夸克对（tt̄W）产生的两圈QCD散射振幅的数值计算，保留了对顶夸克质量和W玻色子质量的精确依赖，并在't Hooft-Veltman方案下实现，与独立实现进行交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tt̄W过程对于精确检验标准模型和探测新物理具有重要意义，其NNLO QCD修正需要两圈振幅。然而全解析计算极其复杂，因此需要开发一种结合数值计算与解析控制的方法来获得精确的两圈振幅。

Method: 采用混合框架：将有限余项表示为具有有理系数的特殊函数之和；特殊函数通过幂级数展开求解微分方程进行数值求值；有理系数通过有限域求值逐点重构。计算在't Hooft-Veltman方案中进行，并与使用传统维数正则化的独立实现交叉验证。

Result: 成功计算了tt̄W两圈振幅，保留了质量依赖，给出了在先前NNLO QCD修正计算中使用的相空间网格上的颜色和极化求和硬函数，并通过两种独立实现的一致性验证了计算的正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为tt̄W过程的NNLO QCD计算提供了关键的两圈振幅输入，证明了混合数值-解析框架在处理高复杂度多尺度两圈振幅方面的有效性，为进一步精确的理论预言奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present a numerical computation of the two-loop QCD scattering amplitudes for the production of a top-antitop quark pair in association with a $W$ boson ($t\bar{t}W$) at the LHC in the generalised leading-colour approximation, retaining the exact dependence on the top-quark and $W$-boson masses. Rather than pursuing a fully analytic calculation, we employ a hybrid framework that combines numerical evaluation with strong algebraic and analytic control, allowing ultraviolet and infrared singularities as well as large intermediate cancellations to be treated exactly. This is achieved by expressing the finite remainder in terms of a set of special functions with rational coefficients. The special functions are evaluated numerically by solving differential equations through power-series expansions, while the values of the rational coefficients are reconstructed, point by point, from finite-field evaluations. The calculation is performed in the 't Hooft-Veltman scheme and validated against an independent implementation in conventional dimensional regularisation employing a substantially different computational strategy. We finally provide the colour- and polarisation-summed hard functions evaluated on the phase-space grid used in a previous computation of the next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to the $t\bar{t}W$ cross section.

</details>


### [170] [Formation of Quark-Gluon Plasma droplets under Ultra-Relativistic Heavy Ion Collisions in the presence of magnetic fields with a modified hadronic medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03774)
*Ananya Sharma,Divya Rathee,Agam K. Jha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于Thomas-Fermi和Bethe模型构建夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）的状态密度，通过计算自由能来系统分析QGP液滴的稳定性，重点考察了化学势、磁场、有效质量项以及介子（π介子和K介子）介质对液滴稳定性的影响。研究发现K介子介质与原有的π介子介质共同作用能增强系统稳定性，化学势与磁场具有相反效应但共同有助于束缚液滴；熵和热容量的计算揭示了系统的相变性质，结果与格点QCD模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在夸克-胶子等离子体研究中，理解QGP液滴（色液滴）的稳定形成条件对于深入认识夸克物质的相结构和极端条件下物质的性质至关重要。此前已有研究基于Thomas-Fermi和Bethe模型建立了QGP各组分的态密度，但尚未系统地考虑化学势、磁场和不同种类介子介质对液滴稳定性的综合影响。

Method: 采用Thomas-Fermi和Bethe模型对QGP各组分的态密度进行建模，引入包含静止质量和热力学质量的有效质量项来刻画夸克和胶子的质量。通过计算系统的自由能并将其作为液滴半径、化学势和磁场的函数进行分析，寻找稳定液滴形成的条件。同时计算熵和热容量以考察系统的相变性质。

Result: 研究发现：（1）在已有的π介子介质基础上引入K介子介质能够为系统提供更大的稳定性；（2）化学势和磁场具有相反的效应，但二者共同作用有助于液滴的束缚；（3）熵和热容量的计算揭示了系统相变的具体性质。所有结果均与格点QCD模拟相符。

Conclusion: 化学势、磁场有效质量以及介子（π和K）介质对QGP液滴稳定性有着关键的综合调控作用，K介子介质增强了系统的稳定性，化学势与磁场的协同作用有助于液滴束缚，分析结果与格点QCD模拟的一致性验证了该模型的可靠性。

Abstract: We build upon the previous studies conducted in this domain using the density of states of various components of the quark-gluon plasma(QGP) modelled after the Thomas-Fermi and Bethe models. This study provides an analysis of the stability of the quark-gluon plasma droplet by the inclusion and variation of chemical potential and magnetic field. An effective mass term is included to account for both the rest mass and thermodynamic mass terms of the quarks and gluon. We also expand on the inclusion of a different meson, kaon, into the medium of the droplet. The analysis on these quantities is done by calculating the free energy of the system and examining it as a function of droplet radius, chemical potential and magnetic field to find conditions for stable droplet formation. We discover that a kaonic medium, along with a pre-existing pionic medium, provides greater stability to the system. At the same time, it is realised that the parameters of chemical potential and magnetic field, due to their opposing effect, help in containing the droplet. Further calculations on entropy and heat capacity yield information about the nature of the phase transition of the system. Our analysis and results are in accordance with lattice QCD simulations.

</details>


### [171] [Formulation of the Deser formula for the shift of energy levels of hadronic atoms in terms of the effective radius of the strong interaction with the Coulomb wave function of hadronic atoms at the origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03795)
*O. Voskresenskaya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了忽略π介子强相互作用时推导出的A_2π原子ns态产生概率关系，在将强相互作用作为一阶微扰考虑后几乎保持不变；并给出了以强相互作用有效力程和库仑波函数原点强相互作用相对修正表示的Deser方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强相互作用对A_2π（双π原子）ns态产生概率关系的影响程度，并完善强子原子（尤其是π介子原子）能级位移的理论描述。

Method: 采用一阶微扰理论处理π介子间的强相互作用，将其与忽略强相互作用的自由情况（库仑相互作用）进行对比；并用有效力程展开和库仑波函数原点修正重新表述Deser方程。

Result: 强相互作用在一阶微扰论下几乎不改变ns态A_2π原子产生概率之间的关系；Deser方程得以用强相互作用有效力程和相对修正的库仑波函数进行重新表述。

Conclusion: 在弱耦合近似下，强相互作用对A_2π原子ns态产生概率关系的影响可忽略，且Deser方程可通过有效力程和波函数修正获得更精确的形式化表达。

Abstract: It is shown that the relations between probabilities of A_2π-atoms creation in ns-states, derived with neglecting of strong interaction between pions, remain practically unchanged if the strong interaction is taken into account in the first order of perturbation theory. The formulation of Deser equation for the energy shift of energy levels of hadronic atoms is given in terms of the effective range of strong interaction and the relative correction to the Coulomb wave function of hadronic atoms at the origin, caused by the strong interaction.

</details>


### [172] [Di-Higgs Production and Trilinear Higgs Self-Coupling in the scNMSSM: Qualitative Implications for the Electroweak Phase Transition at large λ and Low tan\b{eta}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03936)
*Marwa Telba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在半约束NMSSM（scNMSSM）大λ/低tanβ参数区域，以完整双圈精度研究了三线性Higgs自耦合和双Higgs产生截面。对一百万个参数点进行扫描并施加理论、对撞机、味物理和暗物质约束后，得到66个满足SM-like条件的点，全部落在m_{h1}∈[122,125] GeV区间。三线性耦合比普遍压低（κλ=0.884–0.982），非共振双Higgs截面在30.3–36.9 fb范围（0.88–1.05倍SM），共振贡献可忽略。所有预言与ATLAS+CMS现有上限及HL-LHC探测预期一致，并与电弱相变的强化首次相变定性相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究scNMSSM在大λ/低tanβ区域中三线性Higgs自耦合和双Higgs产生截面的行为，特别是其偏离标准模型预言的幅度，并评估该模型中电弱相变强化的可能性。

Method: 对一百万个随机参数点进行扫描，施加理论、对撞机、味物理和暗物质约束；以完整双圈精度计算三线性Higgs自耦合和双Higgs产生截面，并分离非共振与共振贡献；将结果与ATLAS+CMS实验上限对比。

Result: 获得66个SM-like参数点（单态分数S13²<0.05），全部位于m_{h1}∈[122,125] GeV区间。κλ=0.884–0.982（均值0.944±0.018），整体呈现普遍压低；非共振双Higgs截面为30.3–36.9 fb（0.88–1.05倍SM），共振贡献≤0.004 fb可忽略；所有结果与实验约束及HL-LHC探测前景一致。

Conclusion: scNMSSM在大λ/低tanβ区域中对κλ呈现普遍压低，这与可能强化的电弱相变定性一致；精确的相变强度定量分析留待有限温度分析工作。该区域可望在HL-LHC上被探测。

Abstract: The trilinear Higgs self-coupling and di-Higgs production cross section are investigated in the semi-constrained NMSSM (scNMSSM) at large-$λ$ / low-$\tanβ$ with full two-loop precision. A random scan of one million parameter points subject to theoretical, collider, flavor, and dark matter constraints yields 66 SM-like points with singlet fraction $S_{13}^2 < 0.05$, all found in the mass range $m_{h_1} \in [122.0,\,125.0]~\text{GeV}$ --- a feature that reflects an intrinsic property of the scNMSSM at large-$λ$ / low-$\tanβ$, where doublet-dominated configurations are kinematically confined below ${\sim}125~\text{GeV}$. The trilinear coupling ratio is found to be universally suppressed: $κ_λ= 0.884$--$0.982$, $\langleκ_λ\rangle = 0.944 \pm 0.018$. The non-resonant di-Higgs cross section at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6~\text{TeV}$ lies in the range $30.3$--$36.9~\text{fb}$ ($0.88$--$1.05\,σ_{\rm SM}$), with the resonant contribution via $gg \to h_3 \to h_1h_1$ negligible ($\leq 0.004~\text{fb}$). All predictions are consistent with current ATLAS+CMS di-Higgs upper limits and is expected to be probed at the HL-LHC. These results are discussed in the context of the electroweak phase transition, where the universal suppression of $κ_λ$ is qualitatively consistent with a modified Higgs potential that could support a strengthened first-order transition. A quantitative determination of the phase transition strength via finite-temperature analysis is left for future work.

</details>


### [173] [Mono-X Signal Characterization from Two-component Dark Matter Using a Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03975)
*Max Fusté Costa,Yong Sheng Koay,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了卷积神经网络（CNN）在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上通过单喷注和单Z玻色子探针表征双组分暗物质（DM）信号的潜力。研究表明，CNN不仅能推断存在两种DM粒子，还能在探测器级别分析中提取它们的质量和自旋（0或1/2），但这仅作为概念验证，因为尚未进行信号-本底分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双组分暗物质是超出标准模型的重要理论假设，然而现有的实验分析通常假设单一暗物质组分。作者希望利用CNN强大的模式识别能力，判断其能否在LHC的大量事件样本中有效区分和表征两种不同的暗物质粒子信号。

Method: 使用CNN处理来自单喷注（mono-jet）和单Z玻色子（mono-Z）两个探测通道的事件数据。经过探测器级别（detector level）的模拟分析，CNN旨在同时完成两个任务：一是判断是否存在两种不同的暗物质组分，二是精确提取每种暗物质粒子的质量和自旋量子数（自旋为0或1/2）。

Result: CNN能够同时完成上述两个任务：不仅能推断出双组分暗物质的存在，还能成功提取每种暗物质粒子的质量和自旋信息。然而，该结果仅作为概念验证（proof-of-concept），论文尚未进行信号与本底（signal-to-background）的区分分析。

Conclusion: 基于CNN的方案在探测器级别的模拟中展现出表征双组分暗物质信号的可行性，证明了深度学习在该领域的应用潜力。但由于缺乏信号-本底分析，该结果仅作为概念验证，后续工作需进一步评估其在真实实验环境中的灵敏度与可行性。

Abstract: We assess the scope of a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) in characterizing potential signals of two-component Dark Matter (DM) arising at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) from mono-jet and mono-Z probes. We show that such a CNN has the ability of not only inferring the presence of two DM particles but also of extracting their mass and spin, the latter being either 0 or 1/2, following detector level analysis. However, such result represents a conceptual proof-of-concept, as we have not entertained a signal-to-background analysis.

</details>


### [174] [A multi-axion model of inflation and dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26138)
*Marcos A. G. Garcia,Hansel Gordillo-Ruiz,Raul Henriquez-Ortiz,Saul Ramos-Sanchez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有额外维度的模型中额外维度轴子（axion）在紧致化后产生的一系列质量态对宇宙学动力学的影响。这些态在早期驱动暴胀，在晚期表现为动力学暗物质。自然参数选择下暴胀轨迹近似测地线，由最重态有效驱动单一暴胀子，所有暴胀可观测量与观测一致，再加热可持续至几MeV温度。比暴胀子轻的态在物质主导时代才主导能量密度，经历级联衰变为辐射的同时维持足够的暗物质丰度，最轻暗物质候选体的寿命远超宇宙年龄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外维度模型不可避免地在紧致化后产生大量质量态，这些态必然参与宇宙学演化。作者旨在统一解释暴胀与暗物质的起源——利用同一个额外维度轴子框架，同时满足暴胀观测约束和暗物质遗迹密度要求，从而构建一个自洽的、由额外维度物理统一驱动的宇宙学场景。

Method: 作者在紧致化后的额外维度轴子框架下构建宇宙学模型，通过自然参数选择使暴胀轨迹近似测地线，从而将暴胀有效简化为最重态驱动的单场暴胀。使用精确数值求解运动方程来验证暴胀动力学、再加热过程及粒子衰变级联，并对比所有暴胀可观测量（含谱畸变）与当前观测约束。

Result: 所有暴胀可观测量（包括谱畸变）与当前实验观测一致；精确数值解表明再加热可以一致地进行到几MeV温度。比暴胀子轻的所有态在物质主导时代才主导能量密度，作为暗物质发挥作用，经历级联衰变为辐射的同时维持足够暗物质丰度以解释观测的遗迹密度。最轻暗物质候选体在远超宇宙年龄的时间尺度上稳定，满足最保守的观测约束。

Conclusion: 额外维度轴子的质量塔可以统一解释暴胀和暗物质：最重态驱动暴胀，较轻态在晚时表现为暗物质。该框架在中等温度下成功完成再加热，且全部可观测量与观测一致，表明这为额外维度模型中的宇宙学提供了自洽且可检验的理论方案。

Abstract: Models with extra dimensions include a tower of massive states that unavoidably contribute to cosmological dynamics. In particular, after compactification, a higher-dimensional axion gives rise to a set of states whose dynamics at early times exhibit the properties of inflation and, at late times, those of dynamical dark matter. Interestingly, natural choices of parameters imply that the inflationary trajectory in field space is approximately geodesic, meaning that inflation is effectively driven by a single inflaton corresponding to the heaviest state. In this scenario, all inflationary observables, including the emerging spectral distortions, are consistent with current observational constraints, and exact numerical solutions of the equations of motion show that reheating can consistently proceed down to temperatures of a few MeV. Furthermore, all states lighter than the inflaton only come to dominate the energy density of the Universe during the matter-dominated era, thus behaving as dark matter at late times. These states undergo a cascade of decays into radiation, while maintaining a sufficient dark-matter abundance to account for the observed relic density. The lightest dark-matter candidate remains stable on timescales far exceeding the age of the Universe, thereby satisfying even the most conservative observational constraints.

</details>


### [175] [Revised exclusion limits on doubly charged Higgs bosons from a reanalysis of the ATLAS multi-lepton search at $\sqrt{s} = 13$,TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03988)
*Kirtiman Ghosh,Arun Kumar Nayak,Debabrata Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视ATLAS对双双电荷希格斯玻色子搜索（Run 2全数据）的四轻子信号效率，发现其超过了基于等分支比假设、τ轻子衰变分支比和ATLAS轻子重建效率推导出的严格质量无关上限。作者独立重新生成信号并重算排除极限，发现修正后的预期极限比ATLAS报道值系统性高约两倍或更多，导致预期质量下限显著降低（type-II跷跷板模型从1065 GeV降到约950 GeV，Zee-Babu模型从880 GeV降到约770 GeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑ATLAS实验对双双电荷希格斯玻色子搜索中四轻子信号效率的合理性，发现其与物理原理推导的严格上限矛盾，提示ATLAS公布的信号预测可能存在错误，需要独立验证并修正质量排除极限。

Method: 从ATLAS公布的辅助cutflow推断四轻子信号效率，并将其与基于等分支比假设、τ轻子衰变分支比和ATLAS轻子重建效率推导的严格、质量无关上限进行比较。排除强子τ或喷流误判作为可能解释后，独立重新生成信号事件，基于修正后的信号产额、ATLAS背景预测与不确定性，使用pyhf实现的CLs方法重新计算排除极限。

Result: 发现ATLAS的四轻子信号效率超过物理允许的严格上限，且不能通过合理水平的误判率解释。重新计算的预期排除极限比ATLAS报道值系统性高约两倍或更多。左-右对称type-II跷跷板模型中预期质量下限从1065 GeV降至约950 GeV，Zee-Babu模型中从880 GeV降至约770 GeV。

Conclusion: ATLAS原文中四轻子通道的信号效率存在不一致，暗示其信号预测有误。修正后的排除极限显著弱于ATLAS报道的结果，双双电荷希格斯玻色子质量排除下限应大幅下调。

Abstract: The ATLAS search for pair-produced doubly charged Higgs bosons in multi-lepton final states using the full Run 2 dataset [Eur. Phys. J. C 83 (2023) 605] reports the strongest limits to date on the mass of doubly charged scalars, driven by an essentially background-free four-lepton channel. We show that the four-lepton signal efficiency implied by the auxiliary cutflow of that analysis exceeds a strict, mass-independent upper bound derived from the equal-branching-ratio assumption of the search, the leptonic $τ$ branching fractions, and the ATLAS lepton reconstruction efficiencies. We show that this excess cannot be explained by hadronic $τ$ or jet misidentification without invoking fake rates far above realistic values. We regenerate the signal independently and recompute the exclusion limit, using the corrected signal yields, the ATLAS background predictions and uncertainties, and the same $CL_s$ procedure implemented in pyhf. The resulting expected limit lies systematically above the ATLAS expected limit, by roughly a factor of two or more. This shifts the expected lower mass bound from $1065$ GeV to $\sim950$ GeV in the left-right symmetric type-II seesaw model, and from $880$ GeV to $\sim770$ GeV in the Zee--Babu model.

</details>


### [176] [Collider Spin Tomography with Missing Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28346)
*Tengyu Ai,Qi Bi,Yuxin He,Zekun Li,Jia Liu,Xiao-Ping Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在存在不可见粒子导致运动学歧义的情况下，利用粗粒化POVM描述可见测量，并证明信息损失由可见数据映射的零空间决定而非运动学解的数量。针对e+e-→τ+τ-→π+π-+νν-bar过程，证明双重歧义仅使反对称自旋关联组合C_nr-C_rn不可识别，其余十四个自旋系数均可识别。开发了仅依赖可见数据、无需理论模板的自洽不动点展开方法，在标准模型和反常τ偶极矩基准下均能正确重建微分产生率和可识别自旋系数，优于常规平坦平均方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在碰撞器自旋层析中，不可见粒子会造成运动学歧义（多重折叠），传统方法通常对折叠做平坦平均导致有偏重建。作者旨在严格刻画这种信息损失，并提供实用的无偏重建方法。

Method: 1）将不可见粒子造成的运动学歧义下的可见测量形式化为自旋密度矩阵上的粗粒化POVM；2）证明信息损失由可见数据映射的零空间决定；3）提出自洽固定点展开（fixed-point unfolding）方法，仅使用可见数据、不依赖理论产生模板。

Result: 在τ+τ-不可见中微子例子中，双折叠歧义只使C_nr-C_rn不可识别；固定点展开法在标准模型和反常τ偶极矩基准下均正确重建所有可识别量，而平坦平均产生显著偏差。有非平凡零空间时，可识别子空间加正定性约束给出concurrence和CHSH参数的受控范围。

Conclusion: 缺失中微子导致的运动学歧义不会摧毁碰撞器自旋层析。信息损失的根源在于可见数据映射的零空间结构，而非解的数量；所提出的固定点展开方法可在实际中实现无偏的自旋系数重建。

Abstract: Missing neutrinos need not destroy collider spin tomography. We formulate the visible measurement under kinematic ambiguities arising from invisible particles as a coarse-grained positive-operator-valued measure on the production spin density matrix. We show that information loss is governed by the null space of the resulting visible-data map, not by the number of kinematic solutions. In $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-\toπ^+π^-+ν\barν$, the twofold ambiguity leaves only the antisymmetric spin-correlation combination $C_{nr}-C_{rn}$ unidentifiable, while the differential production rate and the remaining fourteen spin coefficients are identifiable. For practical reconstruction under kinematic ambiguities, we develop a self-consistent fixed-point unfolding method using only visible data, without assuming a theoretical production template. Closure tests in Standard Model and anomalous tau-dipole benchmarks show that the method reproduces the truth-level differential production rate and all identifiable spin coefficients, whereas the usual flat average over kinematic folds gives significantly biased reconstructions. When a nontrivial null space is present, the reconstructed identifiable subspace together with positivity yields controlled ranges for concurrence and the CHSH parameter.

</details>
